-- Interface rebuilt on a warm yellow/gold palette.

local Players            = game:GetService("Players")

-- ── Lua version compatibility polyfills ──────────────────────
if not math.clamp then
    math.clamp = function(v, lo, hi)
        if v < lo then return lo elseif v > hi then return hi end
        return v
    end
end
if not math.round then
    math.round = function(v) return math.floor(v + 0.5) end
end
if not typeof then
    typeof = function(v) return type(v) end
end

-- ── Volt / executor compatibility shim ───────────────────────
do
    local genv = getgenv and getgenv()
    local function adopt(name, fallback)
        if _G[name] ~= nil then return end
        local v = rawget(_G, name)
        if v == nil and genv then v = rawget(genv, name) end
        if v == nil and fallback then v = fallback end
        if v ~= nil then _G[name] = v end
    end
    adopt("request")
    adopt("http_request")
    adopt("fireproximityprompt")
    adopt("getconnections")
    adopt("getcustomasset")
    adopt("writefile")
    adopt("readfile")
    adopt("isfile")
    adopt("delfile")
    adopt("setclipboard")
    adopt("getclipboard")
    adopt("getrawmetatable")
    adopt("setreadonly")
    adopt("newcclosure")
    adopt("Drawing")
    if identifyexecutor then
        local ok, id = pcall(identifyexecutor)
        if ok then
            local n = type(id) == "table" and (id.Name or id[1]) or tostring(id or "")
            if tostring(n):lower():find("volt") then
                print("[Verity] Volt detected")
            end
        end
    end
end

local request = http_request or (getgenv and (getgenv().request or getgenv().http_request)) or (syn and syn.request) or (http and http.request) or (fluxus and fluxus.request) or request
if not request then
    warn("[Verity] No HTTP request function — song download may fail, rest of script still runs")
end

end)

    local pg = game:GetService("Players").LocalPlayer
    pg = pg and pg:FindFirstChild("PlayerGui")
    if pg then
        -- Both the current and the pre-rebrand names are cleaned up, so
        -- re-executing never leaves a stale window behind.
        for _, name in ipairs({"VerityDuels", "VerityDuels", "VerityIntro", "VerityTracerReliable"}) do
            local old = pg:FindFirstChild(name)
            if old then old:Destroy() end
        end
    end
end)
pcall(function()
    if _G.AceStopAntiDesyncAimbot then _G.AceStopAntiDesyncAimbot() end
    if _G.AceStopNormalAimbot then _G.AceStopNormalAimbot() end
end)

-- Derive an alternate colorway by rotating only the saturated keys. Surfaces
-- keep their brightness and neutral cast, so a colorway change can never leave
-- half the window tinted the wrong way.
local function deriveScheme(base, hueShift, satScale)
    local out = {}
    for key, value in pairs(base) do
        if typeof(value) == "Color3" then
            local hue, sat, val = Color3.toHSV(value)
            if sat > 0.12 then
                out[key] = Color3.fromHSV(
                    (hue + hueShift) % 1,
                    math.clamp(sat * (satScale or 1), 0, 1),
                    val
                )
            else
                out[key] = value
            end
        else
            out[key] = value
        end
    end
    return out
end

local COLOR_SCHEMES = {
    [BG_YELLOW]   = YELLOW_BASE,
    [BG_LEGACY]   = YELLOW_BASE,
    [BG_AMBER]    = deriveScheme(YELLOW_BASE, -0.026, 0.96),
    [BG_DARKGOLD] = deriveScheme(YELLOW_BASE, -0.042, 0.90),
}

-- YELLOW is the default colorway. GOLD is the alternate, derived from whichever
-- base is active so custom skins keep working.
local function getBrandThemeScheme(baseScheme, mode)
    baseScheme = baseScheme or YELLOW_BASE
    if mode ~= "Gold" then return baseScheme end
    return deriveScheme(baseScheme, -0.026, 0.96)
end

-- ── Brand artwork ────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
-- Both ids below were uploaded as DECALS. Roblox refuses to draw a decal inside
-- an ImageLabel, so each one is turned into something an ImageLabel CAN draw.
-- Resolution order, best first:
--   1. an image id pasted into VERITY.ART_LOGO_IMAGE_ID / ART_BG_IMAGE_ID
--   2. the image id the decal wraps, fetched from assetdelivery (cached)
--   3. rbxthumb:// -- draws the asset thumbnail straight from the asset id
--   4. the public asset-thumbnail URL
-- The whole thing is one closure so it costs the main chunk a single register
-- block; the main chunk is close to Lua's register ceiling.
local ART_LOGO, ART_BG, artFor, setArt = (function()
    local LOGO_DECAL = "130326929969503"
    -- Default VERITY background decal. The old 7up-era legacy background is
    -- separately blocked in artFor below.
    local BG_DECAL   = "104401995376905"

    -- Paste an IMAGE id here to skip the network lookup entirely.
    local LOGO_IMAGE = nil
    local BG_IMAGE   = nil

    local SWAP = {}   -- decal id -> image id  (false = lookup already failed)

    -- Executors expose the HTTP primitive under several names; try them all.
    local function findHttp()
        for _, c in ipairs({ request, http_request, http }) do
            if type(c) == "function" then return c end
            if type(c) == "table" and type(c.request) == "function" then return c.request end
        end
        if type(syn) == "table" and type(syn.request) == "function" then return syn.request end
        return nil
    end

    local function decalToImage(decalId)
        local id = tostring(decalId or ""):gsub("%D", "")
        if id == "" then return nil end
        if SWAP[id] then return SWAP[id] end
        if SWAP[id] == false then return nil end
        local http = findHttp()
        if not http then SWAP[id] = false; return nil end
        local ok, res = pcall(function()
            return http({ Url = "https://assetdelivery.roblox.com/v1/asset?id=" .. id,
                          Method = "GET" })
        end)
        local body = ok and res and (res.Body or res.body)
        if body then
            local imageId = tostring(body):match("<url>%s*[^<]-id=(%d+)")
            if imageId and imageId ~= id then
                SWAP[id] = imageId
                return imageId
            end
        end
        SWAP[id] = false
        return nil
    end

    -- Every way Roblox can be asked to draw the asset, best first.
    local function candidates(rawId, override)
        local out = {}
        local digits = tostring(rawId or ""):match("(%d+)")
        if override then
            local o = tostring(override):gsub("%D", "")
            if o ~= "" then table.insert(out, "rbxassetid://" .. o) end
        end
        if not digits then return out end
        local wrapped = decalToImage(digits)
        if wrapped then table.insert(out, "rbxassetid://" .. wrapped) end
        -- rbxthumb is what Roblox itself uses to draw an asset by id, and it
        -- works for Decals -- the only lookup-free way to render one.
        table.insert(out, "rbxthumb://type=Asset&id=" .. digits .. "&w=420&h=420")
        table.insert(out, "rbxassetid://" .. digits)
        return out
    end

    local LOGO_C = candidates(LOGO_DECAL, LOGO_IMAGE)
    local BG_C   = candidates(BG_DECAL,   BG_IMAGE)

    local logo = LOGO_C[1] or ("rbxassetid://" .. LOGO_DECAL)
    local bg   = BG_C[1]   or ("rbxassetid://" .. BG_DECAL)

    -- Read these out of the executor console if a texture ever looks wrong.
    pcall(function()
        print("[Verity] logo art -> " .. tostring(logo))
        print("[Verity] background art -> " .. tostring(bg))
    end)

    local function artFor(id)
        if id == nil then return id end
        local digits = tostring(id):match("rbxassetid://(%d+)")
        if digits and SWAP[digits] then return "rbxassetid://" .. SWAP[digits] end
        if bg and digits == tostring(BG_DECAL) then return bg end
        -- Retired 7up-era legacy background: never draw it again. Legacy
        -- saves that still reference it fall back to the Verity background.
        if digits == "102557909116203" then return nil end
        if digits == tostring(LOGO_DECAL) then return logo end
        if digits and SWAP[digits] == false then
            return "rbxthumb://type=Asset&id=" .. digits .. "&w=420&h=420"
        end
        return id
    end
    _G._VRTArtFor = artFor

    -- Assigning nil to .Image throws in Roblox and aborts whatever was mid-build.
    -- Every art assignment goes through this instead.
    local function setArt(target, id)
        if not target then return end
        local value = artFor(id)
        if type(value) ~= "string" or value == "" then return end
        pcall(function() target.Image = value end)
    end
    _G._VRTSetArt = setArt

    return logo, bg, artFor, setArt
end)()

-- Background ids stay as palette keys; the art string is resolved separately.
local YELLOW_BACKGROUND = BG_YELLOW
local GOLD_BACKGROUND   = BG_AMBER
local ART_BACKGROUND    = ART_BG   -- resolved above

-- Branding. Change these three lines to re-skin the whole interface.
local BRAND_NAME     = "VERITY DUELS"
local BRAND_SHORT    = "VERITY"
local BRAND_DISCORD  = "discord.gg/verity"
local BRAND_LOGO_ID  = ART_LOGO

-- YELLOW is the only colorway. The retired GOLD scheme folds onto it here.
_G._VRTThemeMode   = "Yellow"
_G._VRTAccentColor = _G._VRTThemeMode == "Gold"
    and Color3.fromRGB(226, 172, 12) or Color3.fromRGB(255, 205, 24)

local currentColorScheme = getBrandThemeScheme(
    COLOR_SCHEMES[BG_YELLOW], _G._VRTThemeMode
)
local currentBgImage = _G._VRTThemeMode == "Gold" and GOLD_BACKGROUND or YELLOW_BACKGROUND

-- Sweeps every image on screen and swaps any raw decal id for its image id.
-- Safety net for assignment sites that bypass artFor().
local function applyArtEverywhere()
    local pg = LP:FindFirstChild("PlayerGui")
    -- MusicPlayer is declared later in the file, so it is looked up by name here.
    local MusicRoot = pg and pg:FindFirstChild("VerityMusicPlayer")
    for _, host in ipairs({ pg and pg:FindFirstChild("VerityDuels"), pg and pg:FindFirstChild("AutoGrab"), MusicRoot }) do
        if host then
            for _, obj in ipairs(host:GetDescendants()) do
                pcall(function()
                    if obj:IsA("ImageLabel") or obj:IsA("ImageButton") then
                        local swapped = artFor(obj.Image)
                        if swapped and swapped ~= obj.Image then obj.Image = swapped end
                    end
                end)
            end
        end
    end
end
_G._VRTApplyArt = applyArtEverywhere

-- One live blend shared by a can's neck and body, so skins/states never split visually.
local function adaptiveAccentSequence(scheme, active)
    scheme = scheme or currentColorScheme or {}
    local deep = scheme.stackBg or scheme.mainDark or Color3.fromRGB(38, 35, 28)
    local base = active and (scheme.stackActive or scheme.main) or (scheme.mainDark or deep)
    local bright = scheme.mainLight or scheme.main or Color3.fromRGB(255, 205, 24)
    local middle = active and (scheme.main or bright) or (scheme.stackBg or base)
    return ColorSequence.new({
        ColorSequenceKeypoint.new(0,deep),
        ColorSequenceKeypoint.new(0.22,bright),
        ColorSequenceKeypoint.new(0.58,middle),
        ColorSequenceKeypoint.new(1,deep),
    })
end


-- ============================================================
-- SHARED FX ENGINE
-- Traveling gold strokes, glitch bursts, toasts, clipboard and circular
-- masking. Everything registers here so ONE RenderStepped loop drives every
-- animated border across every interface in the build.
-- ============================================================
do
    local DEEP_GOLD  = Color3.fromRGB(118, 90, 12)
    local MAIN_GOLD  = Color3.fromRGB(255, 205, 24)
    local LIGHT_GOLD = Color3.fromRGB(255, 240, 158)

    local SPIN, spinConn = {}, nil

    -- Yellow/gold ramp with two bright bands, so a sweep reads as travelling.
    local function goldRamp()
        local s = currentColorScheme or {}
        return ColorSequence.new({
            ColorSequenceKeypoint.new(0.00, DEEP_GOLD),
            ColorSequenceKeypoint.new(0.16, LIGHT_GOLD),
            ColorSequenceKeypoint.new(0.30, s.main or MAIN_GOLD),
            ColorSequenceKeypoint.new(0.52, DEEP_GOLD),
            ColorSequenceKeypoint.new(0.70, s.mainLight or LIGHT_GOLD),
            ColorSequenceKeypoint.new(0.84, s.main or MAIN_GOLD),
            ColorSequenceKeypoint.new(1.00, DEEP_GOLD),
        })
    end

    local function goldAlpha(dim)
        dim = tonumber(dim) or 0.74
        return NumberSequence.new({
            NumberSequenceKeypoint.new(0.00, dim),
            NumberSequenceKeypoint.new(0.16, 0),
            NumberSequenceKeypoint.new(0.34, dim),
            NumberSequenceKeypoint.new(0.70, 0),
            NumberSequenceKeypoint.new(1.00, dim),
        })
    end

    local function ensureSpin()
        if spinConn then return end
        spinConn = RunService.RenderStepped:Connect(function(dt)
            for i = #SPIN, 1, -1 do
                local e = SPIN[i]
                local g = e.gradient
                if not g or not g.Parent then
                    table.remove(SPIN, i)
                else
                    e.pos = (e.pos + dt * e.speed) % 1
                    local ok = pcall(function()
                        g.Offset = Vector2.new(e.pos - 0.5, e.axis)
                    end)
                    if not ok then table.remove(SPIN, i) end
                end
            end
        end)
    end

    -- Attach an animated gold sweep to any UIStroke. Idempotent: calling it
    -- twice on the same stroke just retunes the existing sweep.
    local function travelStroke(stroke, opts)
        if not stroke then return nil end
        opts = opts or {}
        local grad
        -- Reuse an existing UIGradient rather than adding a second one; some
        -- strokes already carry one and others are looked up by name later.
        local ok = pcall(function()
            grad = stroke:FindFirstChild("TravelGradient")
            if not grad then grad = stroke:FindFirstChildOfClass("UIGradient") end
            if not grad then
                grad = Instance.new("UIGradient")
                grad.Name = "TravelGradient"
                grad.Parent = stroke
            end
            grad.Color = goldRamp()
            grad.Rotation = opts.rotation or -26
            grad.Transparency = goldAlpha(opts.dim)
        end)
        if not ok or not grad then return nil end
        -- Marker so a later sweep can tell this stroke is already animated,
        -- without depending on a gradient's name.
        pcall(function() stroke:SetAttribute("VRTGrad", true) end)
        for _, e in ipairs(SPIN) do
            if e.gradient == grad then
                -- Already animated. Only retune when explicitly asked, so the
                -- "gradient everywhere" sweep cannot overwrite a hand-tuned speed.
                if opts.retune then e.speed = opts.speed or 0.34 end
                return grad
            end
        end
        table.insert(SPIN, {
            gradient = grad,
            speed = opts.speed or 0.34,
            pos = opts.phase or 0,
            axis = opts.axis or 0,
        })
        ensureSpin()
        return grad
    end
    _G._VRTTravelStroke = travelStroke

    -- ── circular masking ────────────────────────────────────────────────────
    -- UICorner(1,0) on an ImageLabel rounds the image itself; wrapping in a
    -- clipped disc frame guarantees it on every executor.
    local function roundImage(label, pixels)
        if not label then return nil end
        local corner
        local ok = pcall(function()
            corner = label:FindFirstChild("BrandRound")
            if not corner then corner = label:FindFirstChildOfClass("UICorner") end
            if not corner then
                corner = Instance.new("UICorner")
                corner.Name = "BrandRound"
                corner.Parent = label
            end
            corner.CornerRadius = pixels and UDim.new(0, pixels) or UDim.new(1, 0)
        end)
        if ok then return corner end
        return nil
    end
    _G._VRTRoundImage = roundImage

    -- ── clipboard ───────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
    local function copyToClipboard(text)
        text = tostring(text or "")
        if text == "" then return false end
        for _, fn in ipairs({ setclipboard, toclipboard, writeclipboard }) do
            if type(fn) == "function" then
                if pcall(fn, text) then return true end
            end
        end
        if type(syn) == "table" and type(syn.clipboard) == "table"
           and type(syn.clipboard.set) == "function" then
            if pcall(syn.clipboard.set, text) then return true end
        end
        return false
    end
    _G._VRTCopyText = copyToClipboard

    -- ── toast ───────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
    local function toast(message)
        local host = _G._VRTMainOuter
        if not host then return end
        local pill = Instance.new("TextLabel")
        pill.Name = "VerityToast"
        pill.Size = UDim2.new(0, 300, 0, 34)
        pill.Position = UDim2.new(0.5, -150, 0, -26)
        pill.BackgroundColor3 = currentColorScheme.topBg or Color3.fromRGB(18, 17, 15)
        pill.BackgroundTransparency = 0.06
        pill.BorderSizePixel = 0
        pill.Text = tostring(message or "")
        pill.TextColor3 = currentColorScheme.mainLight or LIGHT_GOLD
        pill.Font = Enum.Font.GothamBold
        pill.TextSize = 12
        pill.TextTruncate = Enum.TextTruncate.AtEnd
        pill.ZIndex = 80
        pill.Parent = host
        Instance.new("UICorner", pill).CornerRadius = UDim.new(1, 0)
        local pillStroke = Instance.new("UIStroke", pill)
        pillStroke.Color = currentColorScheme.main or MAIN_GOLD
        pillStroke.Thickness = 1.2
        pillStroke.Transparency = 0.1
        travelStroke(pillStroke, { speed = 0.7 })
        TweenService:Create(pill, TweenInfo.new(0.24, Enum.EasingStyle.Back, Enum.EasingDirection.Out),
            { Position = UDim2.new(0.5, -150, 0, 14) }):Play()
        task.delay(1.9, function()
            if not pill.Parent then return end
            TweenService:Create(pill, TweenInfo.new(0.26),
                { Position = UDim2.new(0.5, -150, 0, -30), BackgroundTransparency = 1,
                  TextTransparency = 1 }):Play()
            task.delay(0.32, function() if pill.Parent then pill:Destroy() end end)
        end)
    end
    _G._VRTToast = toast

    local function copyLink(text)
        local ok = copyToClipboard(text)
        if ok then
            toast("LINK COPIED  ·  " .. tostring(text))
        else
            toast("COULD NOT COPY  ·  " .. tostring(text))
        end
        return ok
    end
    _G._VRTCopyLink = copyLink

    -- ── glitch ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
    local SCRAMBLE = "!<>_/\\|#@$%&*+=~^0123456789ABCDEFGHJKLMNPQRSTUVWXYZ"
    local function scramble(text)
        local out = {}
        for i = 1, #text do
            local c = text:sub(i, i)
            if c == " " then
                out[#out + 1] = " "
            else
                local r = math.random(1, #SCRAMBLE)
                out[#out + 1] = SCRAMBLE:sub(r, r)
            end
        end
        return table.concat(out)
    end

    local function spawnBar(host, tall)
        local bar = Instance.new("Frame")
        bar.Name = "GlitchBar"
        bar.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, tall and math.random(3, 9) or math.random(1, 3))
        bar.Position = UDim2.new(0, 0, math.random(), 0)
        bar.BackgroundColor3 = (math.random() < 0.55) and LIGHT_GOLD
            or (currentColorScheme.main or MAIN_GOLD)
        bar.BackgroundTransparency = 0.12
        bar.BorderSizePixel = 0
        bar.ZIndex = 62
        bar.Parent = host
        pcall(function()
            Instance.new("UICorner", bar).CornerRadius = UDim.new(0, 2)
        end)
        task.delay(0.07 + math.random() * 0.13, function()
            if bar and bar.Parent then bar:Destroy() end
        end)
    end

    -- ── gradient everywhere ─────────────────────────────────────────────────
    -- Walks every Verity gui and gives each live UIStroke the travelling gold
    -- band, so no interface is left without it. Strokes that are switched off,
    -- or deliberately invisible, are left alone; each stroke keeps the
    -- visibility it already had.
    local WATCHED_GUIS = {}
    local WORKSPACE_SCANS = 0

    -- Every UIStroke gets the sweep, including ones that start switched off or
    -- fully transparent: those are toggled on later, and the gradient needs to
    -- already be there when they are. A disabled stroke renders nothing, so
    -- this cannot change what is on screen until the stroke is actually shown.
    local function strokeWanted(stroke)
        local marked = false
        pcall(function() marked = stroke:GetAttribute("VRTGrad") == true end)
        if marked then return nil end
        if stroke:FindFirstChild("TravelGradient") then return nil end
        local transp = 0
        pcall(function() transp = tonumber(stroke.Transparency) or 0 end)
        -- The stroke keeps the visibility it already had; only the band moves.
        return math.clamp(transp + 0.3, 0.25, 0.95)
    end

    local function hookGui(root)
        if WATCHED_GUIS[root] then return end
        WATCHED_GUIS[root] = true
        pcall(function()
            root.DescendantAdded:Connect(function(desc)
                if not desc:IsA("UIStroke") then return end
                task.defer(function()
                    pcall(function()
                        if not desc.Parent then return end
                        local dim = strokeWanted(desc)
                        if dim then travelStroke(desc, { speed = 0.42, dim = dim }) end
                    end)
                end)
            end)
        end)
    end

    -- Fully-round elements get a gold outline of their own, so the sweep
    -- follows every circle and not just the panels.
    local ROUND_CLASSES = { Frame = true, ImageLabel = true, ImageButton = true,
                            TextButton = true, TextLabel = true }

    local function isFullyRound(obj)
        local corner = obj:FindFirstChildOfClass("UICorner")
        if not corner then return false end
        local cr = corner.CornerRadius
        if not cr then return false end
        local scale = 0
        local ok = pcall(function() scale = tonumber(cr.Scale) or 0 end)
        return ok and scale >= 1
    end

    local function ensureRoundStroke(obj)
        if not ROUND_CLASSES[obj.ClassName] then return false end
        if obj:FindFirstChild("RoundGold") then return false end
        if obj:FindFirstChildOfClass("UIStroke") then return false end
        local size = 0
        pcall(function() size = obj.AbsoluteSize.Y end)
        if size > 0 and size < 7 then return false end   -- a dot, not a badge
        local stroke = Instance.new("UIStroke")
        stroke.Name = "RoundGold"
        stroke.Thickness = 1
        stroke.Transparency = 0.3
        stroke.Color = (currentColorScheme and currentColorScheme.main)
            or Color3.fromRGB(255, 205, 24)
        stroke.Parent = obj
        travelStroke(stroke, { speed = 0.55, dim = 0.5 })
        return true
    end

    local function sweepGui(root)
        if not root then return 0 end
        local n = 0
        pcall(function()
            for _, obj in ipairs(root:GetDescendants()) do
                if obj:IsA("UIStroke") then
                    local dim = strokeWanted(obj)
                    if dim then
                        travelStroke(obj, { speed = 0.42, dim = dim })
                        n = n + 1
                    end
                elseif isFullyRound(obj) then
                    if ensureRoundStroke(obj) then n = n + 1 end
                end
            end
        end)
        hookGui(root)
        return n
    end

    local function gradientEverywhere()
        local hosts, seen = {}, {}
        local function add(g)
            if g and not seen[g] then seen[g] = true; hosts[#hosts + 1] = g end
        end
        local function collectFrom(container)
            if not container then return end
            pcall(function()
                for _, g in ipairs(container:GetChildren()) do
                    if g:IsA("ScreenGui") or g:IsA("BillboardGui") or g:IsA("SurfaceGui") then
                        local n = tostring(g.Name)
                        if n:find("Verity") or n == "AutoGrab" or n:find("VRT") then add(g) end
                    end
                end
            end)
        end
        collectFrom(LP and LP:FindFirstChild("PlayerGui"))
        collectFrom(CoreGui)
        -- Character billboards live in the 3D world, not in a gui container.
        if WORKSPACE_SCANS < 4 then
            WORKSPACE_SCANS = WORKSPACE_SCANS + 1
            pcall(function()
                for _, d in ipairs(workspace:GetDescendants()) do
                    if d:IsA("BillboardGui") then
                        local n = tostring(d.Name)
                        if n:find("Verity") or n:find("VRT") then add(d) end
                    end
                end
            end)
        end
        local total = 0
        for _, g in ipairs(hosts) do total = total + sweepGui(g) end
        return total
    end
    _G._VRTGradientEverywhere = gradientEverywhere

    -- ── background art that follows the main background ─────────────────────
    -- Surfaces that mirror the window background are found by name, so anything
    -- built later is picked up automatically.
    local BG_MIRROR_NAMES = {
        CopyArt = true, ToggleArt = true, ButtonArt = true, PanelArt = true,
        JukeArt = true, StatusArt = true,
    }

    local function bgArtSweep(root)
        if not root then return 0 end
        local n = 0
        pcall(function()
            for _, obj in ipairs(root:GetDescendants()) do
                if BG_MIRROR_NAMES[obj.Name] and obj:IsA("ImageLabel") then
                    -- Round the art to its host's corner so no square edge
                    -- of the image shows past a rounded/circular button.
                    pcall(function()
                        local host = obj.Parent
                        local hostCorner = host and host:FindFirstChildOfClass("UICorner")
                        if hostCorner then
                            local artCorner = obj:FindFirstChild("ArtRound")
                            if not artCorner then
                                artCorner = Instance.new("UICorner")
                                artCorner.Name = "ArtRound"
                                artCorner.Parent = obj
                            end
                            if artCorner.CornerRadius ~= hostCorner.CornerRadius then
                                artCorner.CornerRadius = hostCorner.CornerRadius
                            end
                        end
                    end)
                    local ok = pcall(function()
                        local id = currentBgImage or BG_YELLOW
                        if id ~= obj:GetAttribute("VRTBg") then
                            setArt(obj, id)
                            pcall(function() obj:SetAttribute("VRTBg", id) end)
                        end
                    end)
                    if ok then n = n + 1 end
                end
            end
        end)
        return n
    end

    -- Called whenever the background changes, so every mirrored surface follows.
    local function syncBgArt()
        local pg = LP and LP:FindFirstChild("PlayerGui")
        local n = 0
        n = n + bgArtSweep(pg and pg:FindFirstChild("VerityDuels"))
        n = n + bgArtSweep(pg and pg:FindFirstChild("AutoGrab"))
        n = n + bgArtSweep(pg and pg:FindFirstChild("VerityMusicPlayer"))
        n = n + bgArtSweep(CoreGui)
        return n
    end
    _G._VRTSyncBgArt = syncBgArt

    local GLITCHED = {}

    local function glitchHost(host, opts)
        if not host then return nil end
        opts = opts or {}
        if GLITCHED[host] then return GLITCHED[host] end

        local titleLbl
        pcall(function()
            local content = host:FindFirstChild("MainContent")
            local bar = content and content:FindFirstChild("HeaderBar")
            titleLbl = bar and bar:FindFirstChild("VerityMainTitle")
        end)

        local function burst()
            if not host.Parent then return end
            if _G._VRTDragging then return end
            local base = host.Position
            local jitter = tonumber(opts.jitter) or 5
            local origTitle = titleLbl and titleLbl.Text
            for step = 1, 4 do
                if _G._VRTDragging then break end
                local dx = (math.random() * 2 - 1) * jitter
                local dy = (math.random() * 2 - 1) * (jitter * 0.5)
                host.Position = UDim2.new(
                    base.X.Scale, base.X.Offset + dx,
                    base.Y.Scale, base.Y.Offset + dy
                )
                if step == 2 and titleLbl and origTitle then
                    titleLbl.Text = scramble(origTitle)
                end
                for _ = 1, math.random(1, 3) do
                    spawnBar(host, true)
                end
                task.wait(0.022)
            end
            if not _G._VRTDragging then host.Position = base end
            if titleLbl and origTitle then titleLbl.Text = origTitle end
            task.wait(0.045)
            -- A short trailing twitch sells it without becoming a nuisance.
            if not _G._VRTDragging then
                host.Position = UDim2.new(base.X.Scale, base.X.Offset + 2, base.Y.Scale, base.Y.Offset)
                task.wait(0.03)
                host.Position = base
            end
        end

        task.spawn(function()
            task.wait(tonumber(opts.delay) or (5 + math.random() * 5))
            while host.Parent do
                local enabled = true
                if type(opts.enabled) == "function" then
                    local ok, v = pcall(opts.enabled)
                    if ok then enabled = v end
                end
                if enabled then
                    local ok = pcall(burst)
                    if not ok then end
                end
                task.wait((tonumber(opts.gap) or 7) + math.random() * 6)
            end
        end)

        GLITCHED[host] = true
        return true
    end
    _G._VRTGlitch = glitchHost
end

-- ============================================================
-- AUTO GRAB MODULE (unchanged)
-- ============================================================
local autoGrabModule = (function()
    -- ================================================================
    -- AUTO STEAL MODULE v2 — Normal + Semi modes
    -- ================================================================
    local RunService    = game:GetService("RunService")
    local ReplicatedStorage = game:GetService("ReplicatedStorage")
    local Stats         = game:GetService("Stats")
    local UIS           = game:GetService("UserInputService")
    local TweenService  = game:GetService("TweenService")
    local CoreGui       = game:GetService("CoreGui")

    local Steal = { AutoStealEnabled = false, StealRadius = 10 }
    local selectedStealMode = "Normal"   -- Normal = Auto Grab V3 (80%), Semi = Auto Grab V2
    local StealRadii = { Normal = 63, Semi = 10 }  -- V3 wide ~80% range, V2 tight

    local currentBgImage    = BG_YELLOW
    local currentColorScheme = COLOR_SCHEMES[currentBgImage]

    -- ── StealBar API (shared between Normal and Semi) ─────────────
    local _barGui, _barFrame, _barFill, _barPct, _barBg = nil,nil,nil,nil,nil
    local _barPingLbl, _barFpsLbl, _barStateLbl, _barCarryLbl = nil,nil,nil,nil
    local _barBrandDisc, _barBrandText, _barFillGradient = nil,nil,nil
    local _barCanWash, _barCanGradient = nil, nil
    local _barSmoothPct = 0
    local _barUI = nil
    local _barScale = 1
    local _barFitScale = 1

    _G.StealBar = {
        SetState    = function(s)
            local name = tostring(s or ""):gsub("^%s+",""):gsub("%s+$","")
            if name == "" or name:upper() == "STEALING" or name:upper() == "AUTO GRAB" then
                name = "BRAINROT"
            end
            if _barStateLbl then _barStateLbl.Text = name:upper() end
            _G._VRTGrabState = name:upper()
        end,
        SetProgress = function(p)
            p = math.clamp(tonumber(p) or 0, 0, 1)
            if _barFill then
                _barSmoothPct = _barSmoothPct + (p - _barSmoothPct) * 0.8
                _barFill.Size = UDim2.new(_barSmoothPct, 0, 1, 0)
                _barFill.BackgroundColor3 = currentColorScheme.autoGrabLine or currentColorScheme.main
            end
            if _barPct then _barPct.Text = tostring(math.floor((_barSmoothPct*100)+0.5)).."%" end
            _G._VRTGrabPct = _barSmoothPct
        end,
        Reset = function()
            _barSmoothPct = 0
            _G._VRTGrabPct = 0
            if _barFill then _barFill.Size  = UDim2.new(0,0,1,0) end
            if _barPct  then _barPct.Text   = "0%" end
        end,
    }

    local _barBgImg = nil  -- direct reference for instant background updates

    local function createAutoGrabUI()
        local old = CoreGui:FindFirstChild("VRTStealBarGui")
        if old then old:Destroy() end
        local old2 = (LP:FindFirstChild("PlayerGui") or LP:WaitForChild("PlayerGui")):FindFirstChild("AutoGrab")
        if old2 then old2:Destroy() end

        local gui = Instance.new("ScreenGui")
        gui.Name          = "AutoGrab"
        gui.ResetOnSpawn  = false
        gui.DisplayOrder  = 20
        gui.IgnoreGuiInset = true
        gui.Parent        = LP:WaitForChild("PlayerGui")
        _barGui = gui
        _G._VRTAutoGrabGui = gui

        _barFrame = Instance.new("Frame", gui)
        _barFrame.Name   = "StealBar"
        _barFrame.Size   = UDim2.new(0, 580, 0, 70)
        _barFrame.AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0.5, 0.5)
        _barFrame.Position = UDim2.new(0.5, 0, 0.91, 0)
        _barFrame.BackgroundColor3 = currentColorScheme.mainDark
        _barFrame.BorderSizePixel  = 0
        _barFrame.Active           = true
        _barFrame.ClipsDescendants = true
        _G._VRTAutoGrabFrame = _barFrame
        _barUI = Instance.new("UIScale", _barFrame)
        local barViewport = workspace.CurrentCamera and workspace.CurrentCamera.ViewportSize or Vector2.new(800,600)
        _barFitScale = math.min(1, math.max(0.56, (barViewport.X - 18) / 580))
        _barUI.Scale = _barScale * _barFitScale
        Instance.new("UICorner", _barFrame).CornerRadius = UDim.new(0, 22)
        local fs = Instance.new("UIStroke", _barFrame)
        fs.Color = currentColorScheme.autoGrabLine or currentColorScheme.main
        fs.Thickness = 1.5; fs.ApplyStrokeMode = Enum.ApplyStrokeMode.Border
        fs.Transparency = 0.35
        if _G._VRTTravelStroke then pcall(_G._VRTTravelStroke, fs, { speed = 0.42, dim = 0.6 }) end
        _barBgImg = Instance.new("ImageLabel", _barFrame)
        _barBgImg.Name = "BgImg"
        _barBgImg.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 1, 0)
        setArt(_barBgImg, currentBgImage)
        _barBgImg.ScaleType = Enum.ScaleType.Crop
        _barBgImg.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(8, 8, 10)
        _barBgImg.BackgroundTransparency = 1
        -- Keep the selected background visible instead of burying it under the tint.
        _barBgImg.ImageTransparency = 0.42
        _barBgImg.BorderSizePixel = 0
        _barBgImg.ZIndex = 1
        _barBgImg.Visible = true
        Instance.new("UICorner", _barBgImg).CornerRadius = UDim.new(0, 22)

        -- Panel wash + accent sweep.
        local canWash = Instance.new("Frame", _barFrame)
        _barCanWash = canWash
        canWash.Name = "PanelWash"
        canWash.Size = UDim2.new(1,0,1,0)
        canWash.BackgroundColor3 = currentColorScheme.mainDark
        canWash.BackgroundTransparency = 0.78
        canWash.BorderSizePixel = 0
        canWash.ZIndex = 2
        canWash.Active = false
        Instance.new("UICorner", canWash).CornerRadius = UDim.new(0, 22)
        local canGradient = Instance.new("UIGradient", canWash)
        _barCanGradient = canGradient
        canGradient.Color = adaptiveAccentSequence(currentColorScheme, false)
        canGradient.Transparency = NumberSequence.new({
            NumberSequenceKeypoint.new(0,0.12), NumberSequenceKeypoint.new(0.2,0.06),
            NumberSequenceKeypoint.new(0.75,0.18), NumberSequenceKeypoint.new(1,0.08),
        })
        canGradient.Rotation = 0
        -- A single soft sweep replaces the old can sheen / bubble / rim set.
        local barSweep=Instance.new("Frame",_barFrame)
        barSweep.Size=UDim2.new(0,120,1,0); barSweep.Position=UDim2.new(0,150,0,0)
        barSweep.Rotation=12; barSweep.BackgroundColor3=Color3.fromRGB(255, 205, 24)
        barSweep.BackgroundTransparency=0.9; barSweep.BorderSizePixel=0; barSweep.ZIndex=1
        Instance.new("UICorner",barSweep).CornerRadius=UDim.new(0,22)
        local barSweepFade=Instance.new("UIGradient",barSweep)
        barSweepFade.Transparency=NumberSequence.new({
            NumberSequenceKeypoint.new(0,1),
            NumberSequenceKeypoint.new(0.5,0),
            NumberSequenceKeypoint.new(1,1),
        })

        -- Drag
        do
            local dragging, dragStart, frameStart, dragInput = false,nil,nil,nil
            _barFrame.InputBegan:Connect(function(inp)
                if inp.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton1 or inp.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.Touch then
                    dragging   = true
                    dragStart  = inp.Position
                    frameStart = _barFrame.Position
                    inp.Changed:Connect(function()
                        if inp.UserInputState == Enum.UserInputState.End then dragging = false end
                    end)
                end
            end)
            _barFrame.InputChanged:Connect(function(inp)
                if inp.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseMovement or inp.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.Touch then
                    dragInput = inp
                end
            end)
            UIS.InputChanged:Connect(function(inp)
                if inp == dragInput and dragging then
                    local dx = inp.Position.X - dragStart.X
                    local dy = inp.Position.Y - dragStart.Y
                    _barFrame.Position = UDim2.new(frameStart.X.Scale, frameStart.X.Offset+dx, frameStart.Y.Scale, frameStart.Y.Offset+dy)
                end
            end)
        end

        -- Brand lockup and one large, easy-to-read mode chip.
        _barBrandDisc = Instance.new("Frame", _barFrame)
        _barBrandDisc.Name = "VerityBrandDisc"
        -- Circular brand badge.
        _barBrandDisc.Size = UDim2.new(0,38,0,38)
        _barBrandDisc.Position = UDim2.new(0,64,0,16)
        _barBrandDisc.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(18, 17, 15)
        _barBrandDisc.BackgroundTransparency = 0.15
        _barBrandDisc.BorderSizePixel = 0
        _barBrandDisc.ZIndex = 5
        _barBrandDisc.ClipsDescendants = true
        Instance.new("UICorner",_barBrandDisc).CornerRadius = UDim.new(1,0)
        local brandStroke = Instance.new("UIStroke",_barBrandDisc)
        brandStroke.Color = currentColorScheme.main
        brandStroke.Thickness = 1.2
        brandStroke.Transparency = 0.3
        if _G._VRTTravelStroke then pcall(_G._VRTTravelStroke, brandStroke, { speed = 0.85, dim = 0.55 }) end

        _barBrandText = Instance.new("ImageLabel", _barBrandDisc)
        _barBrandText.Name = "BrandLogo"
        _barBrandText.Size = UDim2.new(1,0,1,0)
        _barBrandText.Position = UDim2.new(0,0,0,0)
        _barBrandText.BackgroundTransparency = 1
        setArt(_barBrandText, BRAND_LOGO_ID)
        _barBrandText.ScaleType = Enum.ScaleType.Crop
        -- Circular brand mark: the disc already clips, this rounds the image.
        -- roundImage lives in the FX block, so it is reached through the
        -- published hook rather than as a local.
        if _G._VRTRoundImage then pcall(_G._VRTRoundImage, _barBrandText) end
        _barBrandText.ZIndex = 6

        local modeChip = Instance.new("TextLabel", _barFrame)
        modeChip.Name = "ModeChip"
        modeChip.Size = UDim2.new(0,80,0,48)
        modeChip.Position = UDim2.new(0,404,0,11)
        modeChip.BackgroundColor3 = currentColorScheme.main
        modeChip.BackgroundTransparency = 0.08
        modeChip.BorderSizePixel = 0
        modeChip.Text = selectedStealMode:upper()
        modeChip.TextColor3 = currentColorScheme.buttonText or Color3.fromRGB(28, 23, 6)
        modeChip.Font = Enum.Font.GothamBlack
        modeChip.TextSize = 14
        modeChip.TextWrapped = true
        modeChip.ZIndex = 6
        Instance.new("UICorner", modeChip).CornerRadius = UDim.new(1, 0)
        _G._VRTStealModeChip = modeChip

        -- Carry belongs on its own mobile control, not inside the Auto Grab HUD.
        _barCarryLbl = nil
        _G._VRTCarryModeChip = nil
        _G._VRTSetCarryStatus = function() end

        -- Scale controls sit on the can's left rim.
        local function makeScaleBtn(symbol, xOff, delta)
            local btn = Instance.new("TextButton", _barFrame)
            btn.Size = UDim2.new(0,22,0,22)
            btn.Position = UDim2.new(0,xOff,0.5,-11)

            btn.BorderSizePixel = 0
            btn.Text = symbol
