    -- VISUAL TAB (updated with Headless, Korblox, Outfit Changer)
    do
        local page = buildPage("Visual", function()
            makeGap(2)
            makeSectionHeader("Backgrounds")
            makeGap(2)

            local bgImages = {
                {id = BG_YELLOW, label = "VERITY"},
            }

            local previewFrame = Instance.new("Frame", currentPage)
            previewFrame.Size = UDim2.new(1, -16, 0, 104)
            previewFrame.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(28, 27, 23)
            previewFrame.BackgroundTransparency = 0.1
            previewFrame.BorderSizePixel = 0
            previewFrame.LayoutOrder = LO()
            mkCorner(previewFrame, 12)
            local previewStroke=Instance.new("UIStroke",previewFrame); previewStroke.Color=currentColorScheme.main; previewStroke.Transparency=0.45; previewStroke.Thickness=1
            local previewGradient=Instance.new("UIGradient",previewFrame)
            previewGradient.Color=ColorSequence.new({
                ColorSequenceKeypoint.new(0, Color3.fromRGB(255, 205, 24)),
                ColorSequenceKeypoint.new(0.5, Color3.fromRGB(150, 116, 14)),
                ColorSequenceKeypoint.new(1, Color3.fromRGB(44, 42, 36)),
            })
            previewGradient.Rotation = 35

            local previewImage = Instance.new("ImageLabel", previewFrame)
            previewImage.Size = UDim2.new(1, -18, 1, -34)
            previewImage.Position = UDim2.new(0, 9, 0, 9)
            previewImage.BackgroundTransparency = 1
            setArt(previewImage, State.bgImage or BG_YELLOW)
            previewImage.ScaleType = Enum.ScaleType.Crop
            previewImage.ZIndex = 2
            mkCorner(previewImage, 12)

            local previewLabel = Instance.new("TextLabel", previewFrame)
            previewLabel.Size = UDim2.new(1, -20, 0, 18)
            previewLabel.Position = UDim2.new(0, 10, 1, -23)
            previewLabel.BackgroundTransparency = 1
            previewLabel.Text = "ACTIVE BACKGROUND"
            previewLabel.TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(255, 255, 255)
            previewLabel.Font = Enum.Font.GothamBlack
            previewLabel.TextSize = 8
            previewLabel.TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left
            previewLabel.ZIndex = 3

            local bgGrid = Instance.new("Frame", currentPage)
            bgGrid.Size = UDim2.new(1, -16, 0, 0)
            bgGrid.BackgroundTransparency = 1
            bgGrid.BorderSizePixel = 0
            bgGrid.LayoutOrder = LO()
            bgGrid.AutomaticSize = Enum.AutomaticSize.Y

            local gridLayout = Instance.new("UIListLayout", bgGrid)
            gridLayout.FillDirection = Enum.FillDirection.Horizontal
            gridLayout.Padding = UDim.new(0, 8)
            gridLayout.HorizontalAlignment = Enum.HorizontalAlignment.Center
            gridLayout.VerticalAlignment = Enum.VerticalAlignment.Center

            for _, bgData in ipairs(bgImages) do
                local btn = Instance.new("TextButton", bgGrid)
                btn.Size = UDim2.new(0, 72, 0, 62)
                btn.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(72, 67, 47)
                btn.BackgroundTransparency = 0.08
                btn.BorderSizePixel = 0
                btn.Text = ""
                btn.ZIndex = 2
                mkCorner(btn, 15)
                local btnStroke=Instance.new("UIStroke",btn); btnStroke.Color=State.bgImage==bgData.id and Color3.fromRGB(255, 255, 255) or Color3.fromRGB(190, 179, 130); btnStroke.Transparency=State.bgImage==bgData.id and 0.08 or 0.5; btnStroke.Thickness=State.bgImage==bgData.id and 2 or 1
                local btnGradient=Instance.new("UIGradient",btn)
                btnGradient.Color=ColorSequence.new({ColorSequenceKeypoint.new(0,Color3.fromRGB(234, 219, 197)),ColorSequenceKeypoint.new(0.12,Color3.fromRGB(175, 143, 9)),ColorSequenceKeypoint.new(0.8,Color3.fromRGB(55, 51, 36)),ColorSequenceKeypoint.new(1,Color3.fromRGB(179, 167, 150))})
                local btnScale=Instance.new("UIScale",btn); btnScale.Scale=1

                local thumb = Instance.new("ImageLabel", btn)
                thumb.Size = UDim2.new(1, -10, 0, 39)
                thumb.Position = UDim2.new(0, 5, 0, 5)
                thumb.BackgroundTransparency = 1
                setArt(thumb, bgData.id)
                thumb.ScaleType = Enum.ScaleType.Crop
                thumb.ZIndex = 3
                mkCorner(thumb, 10)

                local label = Instance.new("TextLabel", btn)
                label.Size = UDim2.new(1, -8, 0, 14)
                label.Position = UDim2.new(0, 4, 1, -17)
                label.BackgroundTransparency = 1
                label.Text = bgData.label
                label.TextColor3 = currentColorScheme.subText
                label.Font = Enum.Font.GothamBlack
                label.TextSize = 8
                label.TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Center
                label.ZIndex = 3

                local skinCap=Instance.new("Frame",btn)
                skinCap.Name="SkinCap"; skinCap.Size=UDim2.new(0,10,0,10); skinCap.Position=UDim2.new(1,-14,0,4)
                skinCap.BackgroundColor3=Color3.fromRGB(224, 180, 49); skinCap.BorderSizePixel=0; skinCap.ZIndex=5
                skinCap.Visible=State.bgImage==bgData.id; mkCorner(skinCap,5)

                if State.bgImage == bgData.id then
                    btn.BackgroundColor3 = currentColorScheme.main
                    btn.BackgroundTransparency = 0.1
                end

                btn.MouseEnter:Connect(function()
                    TweenService:Create(btn, TweenInfo.new(0.1), {BackgroundTransparency = 0.05}):Play()
                    TweenService:Create(btnScale,TweenInfo.new(0.12,Enum.EasingStyle.Back),{Scale=1.05}):Play()
                    setArt(previewImage, bgData.id)
                end)

                btn.MouseLeave:Connect(function()
                    if State.bgImage ~= bgData.id then
                        TweenService:Create(btn, TweenInfo.new(0.1), {BackgroundTransparency = 0.2}):Play()
                    end
                    TweenService:Create(btnScale,TweenInfo.new(0.12),{Scale=1}):Play()
            setArt(previewImage, State.bgImage or BG_YELLOW)
                end)

                btn.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
                    for _, b in pairs(bgGrid:GetChildren()) do
                        if b:IsA("TextButton") then
                            b.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(72, 67, 47)
                            b.BackgroundTransparency = 0.2
                            local oldStroke=b:FindFirstChildOfClass("UIStroke"); if oldStroke then oldStroke.Transparency=0.5; oldStroke.Thickness=1 end
                            local oldCap=b:FindFirstChild("SkinCap"); if oldCap then oldCap.Visible=false end
                        end
                    end
                    btn.BackgroundColor3 = currentColorScheme.main
                    btn.BackgroundTransparency = 0.1
                    btnStroke.Transparency=0.08; btnStroke.Thickness=2; skinCap.Visible=true
                    changeDuelScriptBackground(bgData.id)
                    setArt(previewImage, bgData.id)
                    requestSave()
                end)
            end

            setArt(previewImage, State.bgImage or BG_YELLOW)

            -- ── CUSTOM ART ───────────────────────────────────────────────
            -- Add your own background / logo decals through a popup, then
            -- switch between everything you've added. Wrapped in its own
            -- function on purpose: the register budget is per function
            -- and this tab builder is one large function already.
            ;(function()
                local function artUrlFor(digits)
                    -- The same URL the shipped art resolves to. rbxthumb is the
                    -- only lookup-free way to draw a decal by id.
                    return "rbxthumb://type=Asset&id=" .. tostring(digits) .. "&w=420&h=420"
                end

                -- Decal ids run to 19 digits and overflow a Lua number, so they
                -- stay strings from the box all the way to the Image property.
                local function decalDigits(text)
                    -- Lenient on purpose: accepts bare numbers and full links.
                    return tostring(text or ""):match("(%d%d%d%d%d+)")
                end

                _G._VRTCustomArtBoxes = _G._VRTCustomArtBoxes or {}
                _G._VRTLogoDigits = _G._VRTLogoDigits or tostring(ART_LOGO):match("(%d+)")

                -- Collections filled by the ADD popups. Config loading can
                -- replace them before the first popup ever opens.
                _G._VRTAddedBackgrounds = _G._VRTAddedBackgrounds or {}
                _G._VRTAddedLogos = _G._VRTAddedLogos or {}

                -- ── ADDER POPUP ─────────────────────────────────────────
                -- Paste a decal id, then switch between everything you've
                -- added from the gallery below the input. One shared builder
                -- serves both backgrounds and logos.
                local adderRoot = nil

                local function closeArtAdder()
                    if adderRoot then adderRoot:Destroy(); adderRoot = nil end
                end

                local function openArtAdder(kind)
                    closeArtAdder()
                    local isBg = kind == "bg"
                    local list = isBg and _G._VRTAddedBackgrounds or _G._VRTAddedLogos
                    local titleText = isBg and "ADD BACKGROUND" or "ADD LOGO"
                    local gridHint = isBg
                        and "YOUR BACKGROUNDS  ·  TAP ONE TO SWITCH"
                        or "YOUR LOGOS  ·  TAP ONE TO SWITCH"

                    local function activeDigits()
                        if isBg then
                            return tostring(State.bgImage or ""):match("(%d%d%d%d%d+)")
                        end
                        return tostring(_G._VRTLogoDigits or "")
                    end

                    -- Built-in tiles let the display switch back to the stock
                    -- looks, not just the user-added ones.
                    local builtIns
                    if isBg then
                        builtIns = {
                            {digits = tostring(BG_YELLOW):match("(%d+)"), label = "VERITY", id = BG_YELLOW},
                        }
                    else
                        builtIns = {
                            {digits = tostring(ART_LOGO):match("(%d+)"), label = "DEFAULT", id = nil},
                        }
                    end

                    local function applyEntry(entry)
                        if isBg then
                            if entry.id then
                                -- Built-in backgrounds keep their palette key.
                                changeDuelScriptBackground(entry.id)
                                if previewImage then setArt(previewImage, entry.id) end
                            else
                                local url = artUrlFor(entry.digits)
                                changeDuelScriptBackground(url)
                                if previewImage then setArt(previewImage, url) end
                            end
                        else
                            _G._VRTSetLogoId(entry.digits)
                        end
                        if requestSave then pcall(requestSave) end
                    end

                    local camera = workspace.CurrentCamera
                    local viewSize = camera and camera.ViewportSize or Vector2.new(800,600)
                    local panelWidth = math.clamp(viewSize.X - 28, 262, 312)
                    local panelHeight = math.clamp(viewSize.Y - 30, 344, 404)

                    local dim = Instance.new("TextButton", gui)
                    dim.Name = "ArtAdderPopup"
                    dim.Size = UDim2.new(1,0,1,0)
                    dim.BackgroundColor3 = currentColorScheme.topBg
                    dim.BackgroundTransparency = 0.24
                    dim.BorderSizePixel = 0
                    dim.Text = ""
                    dim.AutoButtonColor = false
                    dim.ZIndex = 130
                    adderRoot = dim

                    local panel = Instance.new("Frame", dim)
                    panel.AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0.5,0.5)
                    panel.Position = UDim2.new(0.5,0,0.5,0)
                    panel.Size = UDim2.new(0,panelWidth,0,panelHeight)
                    panel.BackgroundColor3 = currentColorScheme.stackBg
                    panel.BorderSizePixel = 0
                    panel.Active = true
                    panel.ZIndex = 131
                    mkCorner(panel, 22)
                    local panelGradient = Instance.new("UIGradient", panel)
                    panelGradient.Color = ColorSequence.new({
                        ColorSequenceKeypoint.new(0, currentColorScheme.mainDark),
                        ColorSequenceKeypoint.new(0.38, currentColorScheme.rowBg),
                        ColorSequenceKeypoint.new(1, currentColorScheme.topBg),
                    })
                    panelGradient.Rotation = 22

                    local header = Instance.new("Frame", panel)
                    header.Size = UDim2.new(1,0,0,46)
                    header.BackgroundColor3 = currentColorScheme.mainDark
                    header.BackgroundTransparency = 0.2
                    header.BorderSizePixel = 0
                    header.ZIndex = 132
                    mkCorner(header, 22)
                    local headerFade = Instance.new("UIGradient", header)
                    headerFade.Color = ColorSequence.new(currentColorScheme.main, currentColorScheme.mainDark)

                    local title = Instance.new("TextLabel", header)
                    title.Size = UDim2.new(1,-96,1,0)
                    title.Position = UDim2.new(0,16,0,0)
                    title.BackgroundTransparency = 1
                    title.Text = titleText
                    title.TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(255, 255, 255)
                    title.Font = Enum.Font.GothamBlack
                    title.TextSize = 15
                    title.TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left
                    title.ZIndex = 133

                    local closeBtn = Instance.new("TextButton", header)
                    closeBtn.Size = UDim2.new(0,30,0,30)
                    closeBtn.Position = UDim2.new(1,-40,0,8)
                    closeBtn.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(215, 173, 49)
                    closeBtn.BorderSizePixel = 0
                    closeBtn.Text = "×"
                    closeBtn.TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(255, 255, 255)
                    closeBtn.Font = Enum.Font.GothamBlack
                    closeBtn.TextSize = 18
                    closeBtn.ZIndex = 134
                    mkCorner(closeBtn, 15)

                    local inputBox = Instance.new("TextBox", panel)
                    inputBox.Size = UDim2.new(1,-116,0,28)
                    inputBox.Position = UDim2.new(0,14,0,56)
                    inputBox.BackgroundColor3 = currentColorScheme.inputBg
                    inputBox.BackgroundTransparency = 0.15
                    inputBox.BorderSizePixel = 0
                    inputBox.ClearTextOnFocus = false
                    inputBox.PlaceholderText = isBg and "PASTE BACKGROUND DECAL ID" or "PASTE LOGO DECAL ID"
                    inputBox.PlaceholderColor3 = currentColorScheme.rowSub
                    inputBox.Text = ""
                    inputBox.TextColor3 = currentColorScheme.inputText
                    inputBox.TextSize = 10
                    inputBox.Font = Enum.Font.Code
                    inputBox.TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left
                    inputBox.ZIndex = 132
                    mkCorner(inputBox, 10)
                    local inputPad = Instance.new("UIPadding", inputBox)
                    inputPad.PaddingLeft = UDim.new(0,8)

                    local addBtn = Instance.new("TextButton", panel)
                    addBtn.Size = UDim2.new(0,78,0,28)
                    addBtn.Position = UDim2.new(1,-92,0,56)
                    addBtn.BackgroundColor3 = currentColorScheme.main
                    addBtn.BackgroundTransparency = 0.06
                    addBtn.BorderSizePixel = 0
                    addBtn.Text = "ADD"
                    addBtn.TextColor3 = currentColorScheme.buttonText
                    addBtn.Font = Enum.Font.GothamBlack
                    addBtn.TextSize = 12
                    addBtn.AutoButtonColor = false
                    addBtn.ZIndex = 132
                    mkCorner(addBtn, 10)
                    local addStroke = Instance.new("UIStroke", addBtn)
                    addStroke.Color = currentColorScheme.mainLight or currentColorScheme.main
                    addStroke.Transparency = 0.4
                    addStroke.Thickness = 1
                    if _G._VRTTravelStroke then pcall(_G._VRTTravelStroke, addStroke, { speed = 0.62, dim = 0.6 }) end

                    local hintLbl = Instance.new("TextLabel", panel)
                    hintLbl.Size = UDim2.new(1,-28,0,13)
                    hintLbl.Position = UDim2.new(0,14,0,90)
                    hintLbl.BackgroundTransparency = 1
                    hintLbl.Text = gridHint
                    hintLbl.TextColor3 = currentColorScheme.main
                    hintLbl.Font = Enum.Font.GothamBlack
                    hintLbl.TextSize = 9
                    hintLbl.TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left
                    hintLbl.ZIndex = 132

                    -- The displayer: everything you've added, switchable.
                    local grid = Instance.new("ScrollingFrame", panel)
                    grid.Size = UDim2.new(1,-28,1,-118)
                    grid.Position = UDim2.new(0,14,0,106)
                    grid.BackgroundTransparency = 1
                    grid.BorderSizePixel = 0
                    grid.Active = true
                    grid.ScrollBarThickness = 3
                    grid.ScrollBarImageColor3 = currentColorScheme.main
                    grid.CanvasSize = UDim2.new(0,0,0,0)
                    grid.ZIndex = 132
                    local gridLayout = Instance.new("UIGridLayout", grid)
                    gridLayout.CellSize = UDim2.new(0,72,0,68)
                    gridLayout.CellPadding = UDim2.new(0,6,0,6)
                    gridLayout.HorizontalAlignment = Enum.HorizontalAlignment.Left

                    local emptyLbl = Instance.new("TextLabel", panel)
                    emptyLbl.Size = UDim2.new(1,-28,0,24)
                    emptyLbl.Position = UDim2.new(0,14,0,128)
                    emptyLbl.BackgroundTransparency = 1
                    emptyLbl.Text = "NOTHING ADDED YET"
                    emptyLbl.TextColor3 = currentColorScheme.rowSub
                    emptyLbl.Font = Enum.Font.GothamBold
                    emptyLbl.TextSize = 10
                    emptyLbl.ZIndex = 132

                    local function refreshGrid()
                        for _, child in ipairs(grid:GetChildren()) do
                            if not child:IsA("UIGridLayout") then child:Destroy() end
                        end
                        emptyLbl.Visible = (#list + #builtIns) == 0
                        local active = activeDigits()

                        local function makeTile(entry, removable)
                            local isActive = entry.digits == active
                            local tile = Instance.new("TextButton", grid)
                            tile.BackgroundColor3 = isActive and currentColorScheme.mainDark or Color3.fromRGB(40, 37, 30)
                            tile.BackgroundTransparency = isActive and 0.25 or 0.12
                            tile.BorderSizePixel = 0
                            tile.Text = ""
                            tile.AutoButtonColor = false
                            tile.ZIndex = 133
                            mkCorner(tile, 12)
                            local tileStroke = Instance.new("UIStroke", tile)
                            tileStroke.Color = isActive and currentColorScheme.main or currentColorScheme.rowBorder
                            tileStroke.Transparency = isActive and 0.05 or 0.5
                            tileStroke.Thickness = isActive and 2 or 1

                            local thumb = Instance.new("ImageLabel", tile)
                            thumb.Size = UDim2.new(1,-10,0,40)
                            thumb.Position = UDim2.new(0,5,0,5)
                            thumb.BackgroundTransparency = 1
                            thumb.Image = artUrlFor(entry.digits)
                            thumb.ScaleType = Enum.ScaleType.Crop
                            thumb.ZIndex = 134
                            mkCorner(thumb, 8)

                            local lbl = Instance.new("TextLabel", tile)
                            lbl.Size = UDim2.new(1,-8,0,12)
                            lbl.Position = UDim2.new(0,4,1,-15)
                            lbl.BackgroundTransparency = 1
                            lbl.Text = entry.label or entry.digits
                            lbl.TextColor3 = currentColorScheme.subText
                            lbl.Font = Enum.Font.Code
                            lbl.TextSize = 7
                            lbl.TextTruncate = Enum.TextTruncate.AtEnd
                            lbl.ZIndex = 134

                            if removable then
                                local delBtn = Instance.new("TextButton", tile)
                                delBtn.Size = UDim2.new(0,16,0,16)
                                delBtn.Position = UDim2.new(1,-19,0,3)
                                delBtn.BackgroundColor3 = currentColorScheme.topBg
                                delBtn.BackgroundTransparency = 0.2
                                delBtn.BorderSizePixel = 0
                                delBtn.Text = "✕"
                                delBtn.TextColor3 = currentColorScheme.mainLight
                                delBtn.Font = Enum.Font.GothamBold
                                delBtn.TextSize = 9
                                delBtn.ZIndex = 136
                                mkCorner(delBtn, 8)
                                delBtn.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
                                    for idx, stored in ipairs(list) do
                                        if stored == entry.digits then table.remove(list, idx); break end
                                    end
                                    if requestSave then pcall(requestSave) end
                                    if _G._VRTToast then
                                        pcall(_G._VRTToast, isBg and "BACKGROUND REMOVED" or "LOGO REMOVED")
                                    end
                                    refreshGrid()
                                end)
                            end

                            tile.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
                                applyEntry(entry)
                                if _G._VRTToast then
                                    pcall(_G._VRTToast, isBg and "BACKGROUND SWITCHED" or "LOGO SWITCHED")
                                end
                                refreshGrid()
                            end)
                        end

                        for _, entry in ipairs(builtIns) do makeTile(entry, false) end
                        for _, digits in ipairs(list) do makeTile({digits = digits, label = digits}, true) end

                        local rows = math.ceil((#builtIns + #list) / 3)
                        grid.CanvasSize = UDim2.new(0, 0, 0, rows > 0 and (rows * 74) or 0)
                    end

                    local function addFromInput()
                        local digits = decalDigits(inputBox.Text)
                        if not digits then
                            if _G._VRTToast then
                                pcall(_G._VRTToast, "DIGITS ONLY  ·  PASTE THE DECAL NUMBERS")
                            end
                            return
                        end
                        local isNew = not table.find(list, digits)
                        if isNew then table.insert(list, 1, digits) end
                        inputBox.Text = ""
                        applyEntry({digits = digits, label = digits})
                        if _G._VRTToast then
                            if isNew then
                                pcall(_G._VRTToast, isBg and "BACKGROUND ADDED" or "LOGO ADDED")
                            else
                                pcall(_G._VRTToast, "ALREADY ADDED  ·  APPLIED")
                            end
                        end
                        refreshGrid()
                    end

                    addBtn.MouseButton1Click:Connect(addFromInput)
                    inputBox.FocusLost:Connect(function(enter) if enter then addFromInput() end end)
                    closeBtn.MouseButton1Click:Connect(closeArtAdder)
                    dim.MouseButton1Click:Connect(closeArtAdder)
                    refreshGrid()
                end

                -- Swaps the logo on everything already on screen.
                _G._VRTSetLogoId = function(id)
                    local digits = decalDigits(id)
                    if not digits then return false, "LOGO NEEDS THE DECAL'S NUMBERS" end
                    local url = artUrlFor(digits)
                    local old = tostring(_G._VRTLogoDigits or "")
                    local swapped = 0
                    local pg = LP:FindFirstChild("PlayerGui")
                    local roots = { pg }
                    local char = LP.Character
                    if char then roots[#roots + 1] = char end
                    for _, root in ipairs(roots) do
                        if root then
                            for _, obj in ipairs(root:GetDescendants()) do
                                if obj:IsA("ImageLabel") then
                                    local cur = obj.Image
                                    if type(cur) == "string" and old ~= ""
                                       and cur:find(old, 1, true) then
                                        setArt(obj, url)
                                        pcall(function() obj:SetAttribute("VRTLogoId", digits) end)
                                        swapped = swapped + 1
                                    end
                                end
                            end
                        end
                    end
                    -- Rebind the file-scope name, so anything built later --
                    -- a respawned head billboard, a new mobile button -- picks
                    -- the new logo up on its own.
                    BRAND_LOGO_ID = url
                    _G._VRTLogoImage = url
                    _G._VRTLogoDigits = digits
                    if requestSave then pcall(requestSave) end
                    return true, "LOGO CHANGED  -  " .. swapped .. " SPOTS"
                end

                -- ── ADD rows: current id on display, ADD button beside it ──
                -- previewDigits (logo row) renders a live mini displayer of
                -- the current logo right on the row. _G._VRTSetLogoId sweeps
                -- every ImageLabel carrying the old logo digits, so this tile
                -- follows every logo switch automatically.
                local function makeAdderRow(head, sub, currentId, openFn, previewDigits)
                    local row = Instance.new("Frame", currentPage)
                    row.Size = UDim2.new(1,-16,0,68)
                    row.BackgroundColor3 = currentColorScheme.rowBg
                    row.BackgroundTransparency = 0.7
                    row.BorderSizePixel = 0
                    row.LayoutOrder = LO()
                    mkCorner(row, 10)
                    local rowStroke = Instance.new("UIStroke", row)
                    rowStroke.Color = currentColorScheme.rowBorder
                    rowStroke.Transparency = 0.62
                    rowStroke.Thickness = 1

                    local headLbl = Instance.new("TextLabel", row)
                    headLbl.Size = UDim2.new(1,-16,0,14); headLbl.Position = UDim2.new(0,8,0,5)
                    headLbl.BackgroundTransparency = 1; headLbl.Text = head
                    headLbl.TextColor3 = currentColorScheme.main
                    headLbl.Font = Enum.Font.GothamBold; headLbl.TextSize = 11
                    headLbl.TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left; headLbl.ZIndex = 4

                    local hintLbl = Instance.new("TextLabel", row)
                    hintLbl.Size = UDim2.new(1,-16,0,11); hintLbl.Position = UDim2.new(0,8,0,20)
                    hintLbl.BackgroundTransparency = 1; hintLbl.Text = sub
                    hintLbl.TextColor3 = currentColorScheme.rowSub
                    hintLbl.Font = Enum.Font.Gotham; hintLbl.TextSize = 8
                    hintLbl.TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left; hintLbl.ZIndex = 4
                    hintLbl.TextWrapped = true

                    -- Live logo displayer: a small always-visible tile that
                    -- shows whatever logo is equipped right now.
                    local hasPreview = previewDigits ~= nil and previewDigits ~= ""
                    if hasPreview then
                        local preview = Instance.new("ImageLabel", row)
                        preview.Name = "LogoPreview"
                        preview.Size = UDim2.new(0,24,0,24); preview.Position = UDim2.new(0,8,0,37)
                        preview.BackgroundColor3 = currentColorScheme.inputBg
                        preview.BackgroundTransparency = 0.35
                        preview.BorderSizePixel = 0
                        preview.Image = artUrlFor(previewDigits)
                        preview.ScaleType = Enum.ScaleType.Fit
                        preview.ZIndex = 5
                        mkCorner(preview, 6)
                    end

                    -- Read-only look at the id that is live right now.
                    local displayLbl = Instance.new("TextLabel", row)
                    displayLbl.Size = UDim2.new(1, hasPreview and -114 or -84, 0, 24)
                    displayLbl.Position = UDim2.new(0, hasPreview and 38 or 8, 0, 37)
                    displayLbl.BackgroundColor3 = currentColorScheme.inputBg
                    displayLbl.BackgroundTransparency = 0.15
                    displayLbl.BorderSizePixel = 0
                    displayLbl.Text = currentId ~= "" and currentId or "DEFAULT"
                    displayLbl.TextColor3 = currentColorScheme.inputText
                    displayLbl.TextSize = 10; displayLbl.Font = Enum.Font.Code
                    displayLbl.TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left
                    displayLbl.TextTruncate = Enum.TextTruncate.AtEnd
                    displayLbl.ZIndex = 4
                    mkCorner(displayLbl, 8)
                    local dispPad = Instance.new("UIPadding", displayLbl)
                    dispPad.PaddingLeft = UDim.new(0,8)

                    local addBtn = Instance.new("TextButton", row)
                    addBtn.Size = UDim2.new(0,60,0,24); addBtn.Position = UDim2.new(1,-68,0,37)
                    addBtn.BackgroundColor3 = currentColorScheme.main
                    addBtn.BackgroundTransparency = 0.08
                    addBtn.BorderSizePixel = 0; addBtn.Text = "+ ADD"
                    addBtn.TextColor3 = currentColorScheme.buttonText
                    addBtn.Font = Enum.Font.GothamBold; addBtn.TextSize = 10
                    addBtn.AutoButtonColor = false
                    addBtn.ZIndex = 4
                    mkCorner(addBtn, 12)
                    local btnStroke = Instance.new("UIStroke", addBtn)
                    btnStroke.Color = currentColorScheme.mainLight or currentColorScheme.main
                    btnStroke.Transparency = 0.45; btnStroke.Thickness = 1
                    if _G._VRTTravelStroke then pcall(_G._VRTTravelStroke, btnStroke, { speed = 0.62, dim = 0.6 }) end

                    addBtn.MouseButton1Click:Connect(openFn)
                end

                makeAdderRow(
                    "Background Decal ID",
                    "Opens the gallery: add a new background or switch between saved ones",
                    tostring(State.bgImage or ""):match("(%d%d%d%d%d+)") or "",
                    function() openArtAdder("bg") end)

                makeAdderRow(
                    "Logo Decal ID",
                    "Opens the gallery: add a new logo or switch between saved ones",
                    tostring(_G._VRTLogoDigits or ""),
                    function() openArtAdder("logo") end,
                    tostring(_G._VRTLogoDigits or ""))
            end)()
            makeGap(4)

            makeGap(8)
            makeSectionHeader("Visual Settings")
            makeGap(2)
            
            antiLagSetter = makeToggleRow("Anti-Lag", State.antiLagEnabled, function(on)
                State.antiLagEnabled = on
                if on then enableAntiLag() else disableAntiLag() end
                requestSave()
            end)
            toggleSetters["antiLag"] = antiLagSetter

            local satSetter = makeToggleRow("Saturated Colors", State.saturatedColorsEnabled, function(on)
                State.saturatedColorsEnabled = on
                if on then enableSaturatedColors() else disableSaturatedColors() end
                requestSave()
            end)
            toggleSetters["saturatedColors"] = satSetter

            makeGap(4)
            makeSectionHeader("Field of View")
            makeGap(2)
            
            makeInputRow("FOV (Normal)", _G._VezyFOV or 70, function(n)
                if n >= 70 and n <= 180 then
                    _G._VezyFOV = n
                    local cam = workspace.CurrentCamera
                    if cam and not State.stretchedResEnabled then
                        pcall(function() cam.FieldOfView = n end)
                    end
                    requestSave()
                end
            end)

            makeGap(4)
            makeSectionHeader("Wide Stretch")
            makeGap(2)
            
            stretchSetter = makeToggleRow("Stretch Rez", State.stretchedResEnabled, function(on)
                State.stretchedResEnabled = on
                if on then enableStretchRez() else disableStretchRez() end
                requestSave()
            end)
            toggleSetters["stretchedRes"] = stretchSetter

            makeInputRow("Stretch Value", State.stretchValue or 0.7, function(n)
                n = math.clamp(tonumber(n) or 0.7, 0.3, 1.5)
                State.stretchValue = n
                if State.stretchedResEnabled then
                    disableStretchRez()
                    enableStretchRez()
                end
                requestSave()
            end)

            makeGap(4)
            makeSectionHeader("Character Visuals")
            makeGap(2)
            
            removeAccSetter = makeToggleRow("Remove Accessories", State.removeAcc, function(on)
                State.removeAcc = on
                if not on then
                    if _G._removeAccStop then _G._removeAccStop() end
                end
                if State.customSkinEnabled then pcall(applyCustomSkinToChar, LP.Character) end
                if on then
                    if _G._removeAccStart then _G._removeAccStart() end
                    -- ApplyDescription may stream the catalog accessory back
                    -- a few frames later. Keep purging only the skin-added hat
                    -- during that short window.
                    task.spawn(function()
                        for _ = 1, 16 do
                            if not State.removeAcc then break end
                            pcall(removeCharAccessories, LP.Character)
                            task.wait(0.12)
                        end
                    end)
                end
                requestSave()
            end)
            toggleSetters["removeAcc"] = removeAccSetter

            -- ── NEW: Headless Toggle ──────────────────────────────
            local headlessSetter = makeToggleRow("Headless", State.headlessEnabled, function(on)
                State.headlessEnabled = on
                applyCharterToChar(LP.Character)
                requestSave()
            end)
            toggleSetters["headless"] = headlessSetter

            -- ── NEW: Korblox Toggle ──────────────────────────────
            local korbloxSetter = makeToggleRow("Korblox", State.korbloxEnabled, function(on)
                State.korbloxEnabled = on
                applyCharterToChar(LP.Character)
                requestSave()
            end)
            toggleSetters["korblox"] = korbloxSetter

            -- Custom skin preview/apply popup
            do
                local skinRow = Instance.new("Frame", currentPage)
                skinRow.Size = UDim2.new(1,-16,0,58)
                skinRow.BackgroundColor3 = currentColorScheme.rowBg
                skinRow.BackgroundTransparency = 0.08
                skinRow.BorderSizePixel = 0
                skinRow.LayoutOrder = LO()
                mkCorner(skinRow, 15)
                local skinRowGradient = Instance.new("UIGradient", skinRow)
                skinRowGradient.Color = ColorSequence.new({
                    ColorSequenceKeypoint.new(0, currentColorScheme.mainDark),
                    ColorSequenceKeypoint.new(0.55, currentColorScheme.rowBg),
                    ColorSequenceKeypoint.new(1, currentColorScheme.stackBg),
                })
                skinRowGradient.Rotation = 8

                local skinTitle = Instance.new("TextLabel", skinRow)
                skinTitle.Size = UDim2.new(1,-112,0,24)
                skinTitle.Position = UDim2.new(0,14,0,7)
                skinTitle.BackgroundTransparency = 1
                skinTitle.Text = "CUSTOM SKINS"
                skinTitle.TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(251, 247, 239)
                skinTitle.Font = Enum.Font.GothamBlack
                skinTitle.TextSize = 14
                skinTitle.TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left

                local skinStatus = Instance.new("TextLabel", skinRow)
                skinStatus.Size = UDim2.new(1,-112,0,17)
                skinStatus.Position = UDim2.new(0,14,0,31)
                skinStatus.BackgroundTransparency = 1
                skinStatus.Font = Enum.Font.GothamBold
                skinStatus.TextSize = 10
                skinStatus.TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left

                local skinOpen = Instance.new("TextButton", skinRow)
                skinOpen.Size = UDim2.new(0,82,0,34)
                skinOpen.Position = UDim2.new(1,-94,0.5,-17)
                skinOpen.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(225, 181, 49)
                skinOpen.BorderSizePixel = 0
                skinOpen.Text = "PREVIEW"
                skinOpen.TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(255, 255, 255)
                skinOpen.Font = Enum.Font.GothamBlack
                skinOpen.TextSize = 11
                skinOpen.AutoButtonColor = false
                skinOpen.ZIndex = 5
                mkCorner(skinOpen, 12)

                local activeSkinPopup = nil
                local function refreshSkinRow()
                    local on = State.customSkinEnabled == true
                    local selected = tonumber(State.customSkinVariant) or 1
                    if selected ~= 1 and selected ~= 2 and selected ~= 3 then selected = 1 end
                    local skinName = CUSTOM_SKIN_VARIANTS[selected].name
                    skinStatus.Text = on and (skinName .. " EQUIPPED • TAP TO VIEW")
                        or (skinName .. " READY • TAP TO PREVIEW")
                    skinStatus.TextColor3 = on and currentColorScheme.mainLight or currentColorScheme.subText
                    skinOpen.Text = on and "ACTIVE" or "PREVIEW"
                    skinOpen.BackgroundColor3 = on and currentColorScheme.main or currentColorScheme.mainDark
                end
                toggleSetters["customSkin"] = function()
                    refreshSkinRow()
                    if activeSkinPopup and activeSkinPopup.sync then activeSkinPopup.sync() end
                end
                refreshSkinRow()

                local function setCustomSkinEnabled(on)
                    State.customSkinEnabled = on == true
                    syncCustomSkinVariant(State.customSkinVariant)
                    applyCharterToChar(LP.Character)
                    refreshSkinRow()
                    if activeSkinPopup and activeSkinPopup.sync then activeSkinPopup.sync() end
                    if activeSkinPopup and activeSkinPopup.refreshPreview then
                        activeSkinPopup.refreshPreview()
                    end
                    requestSave()
                end

                local function closeSkinPopup()
                    if activeSkinPopup and activeSkinPopup.dragConn then
                        activeSkinPopup.dragConn:Disconnect()
                    end
                    if activeSkinPopup and activeSkinPopup.root then
                        activeSkinPopup.root:Destroy()
                    end
                    activeSkinPopup = nil
                end

                local function openSkinPopup()
                    if activeSkinPopup and activeSkinPopup.root and activeSkinPopup.root.Parent then
                        activeSkinPopup.root.Visible = true
                        if activeSkinPopup.refreshPreview then activeSkinPopup.refreshPreview() end
                        return
                    end

                    local camera = workspace.CurrentCamera
                    local viewSize = camera and camera.ViewportSize or Vector2.new(800,600)
                    -- Trimmed panel: the popup sits a little lighter on screen.
                    local panelWidth = math.clamp(viewSize.X - 28, 252, 300)
                    local panelHeight = math.clamp(viewSize.Y - 30, 344, 392)

                    local dim = Instance.new("TextButton", gui)
                    dim.Name = "CustomSkinPopup"
                    dim.Size = UDim2.new(1,0,1,0)
                    dim.BackgroundColor3 = currentColorScheme.topBg
                    dim.BackgroundTransparency = 0.24
                    dim.BorderSizePixel = 0

                        ColorSequenceKeypoint.new(0, currentColorScheme.mainDark),

                    header.BackgroundColor3 = currentColorScheme.mainDark
                    header.BackgroundTransparency = 0.2
                    header.BorderSizePixel = 0
                    header.ZIndex = 122
                    mkCorner(header, 22)

                    local logoDisc = Instance.new("Frame", header)

                    logoDisc.Size = UDim2.new(0,38,0,38)
                    logoDisc.Position = UDim2.new(0,12,0,7)

                    local title = Instance.new("TextLabel", header)

                    title.Position = UDim2.new(0,68,0,8)
                    title.BackgroundTransparency = 1
                    title.Text = "CUSTOM SKINS"
                    title.TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(255, 255, 255)
                    title.Font = Enum.Font.GothamBlack
                    title.TextSize = 15
                    title.TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left
                    title.ZIndex = 123

                    local subtitle = Instance.new("TextLabel", header)

                    subtitle.Position = UDim2.new(0,68,0,31)
                    subtitle.BackgroundTransparency = 1
                    subtitle.Text = "YOUR CURRENT AVATAR"
                    subtitle.TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(235, 223, 174)
                    subtitle.Font = Enum.Font.GothamBold
                    subtitle.TextSize = 9
                    subtitle.TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left
                    subtitle.ZIndex = 123

                    local closeBtn = Instance.new("TextButton", header)
                    closeBtn.Size = UDim2.new(0,34,0,34)

                    closeBtn.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(215, 173, 49)

                    variantHolder.BorderSizePixel = 0

                    mkCorner(variantHolder, 12)

                    local tabGradients = {}
                    local function makeSkinVariantButton(text, position, index)
                        local button = Instance.new("TextButton", variantHolder)
                        button.Size = UDim2.new(1/3,-5,1,-6)
                        button.Position = position
                        button.BackgroundColor3 = currentColorScheme.mainDark

                        -- warm gradient plus the travelling gold stroke band.
                        local gradient = Instance.new("UIGradient", button)
                        gradient.Name = "TabGradient"
                        gradient.Rotation = 90
                        gradient.Color = adaptiveAccentSequence(currentColorScheme, false)
                        tabGradients[index] = gradient
                        local stroke = Instance.new("UIStroke", button)
                        stroke.Color = currentColorScheme.main
                        stroke.Thickness = 1.2
                        stroke.Transparency = 0.45
                        if _G._VRTTravelStroke then pcall(_G._VRTTravelStroke, stroke, { speed = 0.62, dim = 0.6 }) end

                    end
                    -- Three tabs. CUSTOM either shows the saved custom fit or
                    -- opens the id editor when nothing is saved yet.
                    local skinOneButton = makeSkinVariantButton("VERITY", UDim2.new(0,3,0,3), 1)
                    local skinTwoButton = makeSkinVariantButton("BART", UDim2.new(1/3,0,0,3), 2)
                    local skinCustomButton = makeSkinVariantButton("CUSTOM", UDim2.new(2/3,-3,0,3), 3)

                    -- EDIT sits beside the tabs and reopens the cloth id editor
                    -- so a saved custom fit can be changed at any time.
                    local editBtn = Instance.new("TextButton", panel)
                    editBtn.Name = "SkinEditButton"
                    editBtn.Size = UDim2.new(0,56,0,30)
                    editBtn.Position = UDim2.new(1,-70,0,58)
                    editBtn.BackgroundColor3 = currentColorScheme.mainDark
                    editBtn.BackgroundTransparency = 0.08
                    editBtn.BorderSizePixel = 0
                    editBtn.Text = "EDIT"
                    editBtn.TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(255, 255, 255)
                    editBtn.Font = Enum.Font.GothamBlack
                    editBtn.TextSize = 10
                    editBtn.AutoButtonColor = false
                    editBtn.ZIndex = 124
                    mkCorner(editBtn, 10)
                    local editGradient = Instance.new("UIGradient", editBtn)
                    editGradient.Name = "TabGradient"
                    editGradient.Rotation = 90
                    editGradient.Color = adaptiveAccentSequence(currentColorScheme, false)
                    local editStroke = Instance.new("UIStroke", editBtn)
                    editStroke.Color = currentColorScheme.main
                    editStroke.Thickness = 1.2
                    editStroke.Transparency = 0.45
                    if _G._VRTTravelStroke then pcall(_G._VRTTravelStroke, editStroke, { speed = 0.62, dim = 0.6 }) end

                    -- ── CUSTOM FIT editor ─────────────────────────────────────
                    -- Opens instead of the avatar preview: just the two cloth
                    -- id fields and a SAVE button, no classic Roblox character.
                    -- Saving closes it and lands on the CUSTOM tab wearing the
                    -- new fit.
                    local editor = Instance.new("Frame", panel)
                    editor.Name = "SkinClothEditor"
                    editor.Size = UDim2.new(1,-28,1,-158)
                    editor.Position = UDim2.new(0,14,0,94)
                    editor.BackgroundColor3 = currentColorScheme.rowBg
                    editor.BackgroundTransparency = 0.08
                    editor.BorderSizePixel = 0
                    editor.ClipsDescendants = true
                    editor.Visible = false
                    editor.ZIndex = 122
                    mkCorner(editor, 17)
                    local editorGradient = Instance.new("UIGradient", editor)
                    editorGradient.Color = ColorSequence.new({
                        ColorSequenceKeypoint.new(0, currentColorScheme.topBg),
                        ColorSequenceKeypoint.new(0.52, currentColorScheme.mainDark),
                        ColorSequenceKeypoint.new(1, currentColorScheme.stackBg),
                    })
                    editorGradient.Rotation = 15

                    local editorTitle = Instance.new("TextLabel", editor)
                    editorTitle.Size = UDim2.new(1,-16,0,16)
                    editorTitle.Position = UDim2.new(0,10,0,8)
                    editorTitle.BackgroundTransparency = 1

                    editorTitle.Font = Enum.Font.GothamBlack
                    editorTitle.TextSize = 12
                    editorTitle.TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left
                    editorTitle.ZIndex = 123

                    local editorHint = Instance.new("TextLabel", editor)
                    editorHint.Size = UDim2.new(1,-16,0,11)
                    editorHint.Position = UDim2.new(0,10,0,24)
                    editorHint.BackgroundTransparency = 1
                    editorHint.Text = "PASTE YOUR CLASSIC CLOTH IDS, THEN SAVE"
                    editorHint.TextColor3 = currentColorScheme.subText
                    editorHint.Font = Enum.Font.GothamBold
                    editorHint.TextSize = 8
                    editorHint.TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left
                    editorHint.ZIndex = 123

                    local function makeEditorField(labelText, placeholder, top)
                        local label = Instance.new("TextLabel", editor)
                        label.Size = UDim2.new(1,-16,0,12)
                        label.Position = UDim2.new(0,10,0,top)
                        label.BackgroundTransparency = 1
                        label.Text = labelText
                        label.TextColor3 = currentColorScheme.mainLight
                        label.Font = Enum.Font.GothamBlack
                        label.TextSize = 9
                        label.TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left
                        label.ZIndex = 123
                        local box = Instance.new("TextBox", editor)
                        box.Size = UDim2.new(1,-20,0,26)
                        box.Position = UDim2.new(0,10,0,top + 13)
                        box.BackgroundColor3 = currentColorScheme.inputBg
                        box.BackgroundTransparency = 0.15
                        box.BorderSizePixel = 0
                        box.ClearTextOnFocus = false
                        box.PlaceholderText = placeholder
                        box.PlaceholderColor3 = currentColorScheme.rowSub
                        box.Text = ""
                        box.TextColor3 = currentColorScheme.inputText
                        box.TextSize = 10
                        box.Font = Enum.Font.Code
                        box.TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left
                        box.ZIndex = 123
                        mkCorner(box, 8)
                        local pad = Instance.new("UIPadding", box)
                        pad.PaddingLeft = UDim.new(0,8)
                        return box
                    end
                    local shirtBox = makeEditorField("CLASSIC SHIRTS ID", "CLASSIC SHIRTS ID", 42)
                    local pantsBox = makeEditorField("CLASSIC PANTS ID", "CLASSIC PANTS ID", 82)

                    local saveBtn = Instance.new("TextButton", editor)
                    saveBtn.Size = UDim2.new(1,-92,0,30)
                    saveBtn.Position = UDim2.new(0,10,1,-40)
                    saveBtn.BackgroundColor3 = currentColorScheme.main
                    saveBtn.BackgroundTransparency = 0.06
                    saveBtn.BorderSizePixel = 0
                    saveBtn.Text = "SAVE"
                    saveBtn.TextColor3 = currentColorScheme.buttonText
                    saveBtn.Font = Enum.Font.GothamBlack
                    saveBtn.TextSize = 12
                    saveBtn.AutoButtonColor = false
                    saveBtn.ZIndex = 123
                    mkCorner(saveBtn, 12)

                    local backBtn = Instance.new("TextButton", editor)
                    backBtn.Size = UDim2.new(0,62,0,30)
                    backBtn.Position = UDim2.new(1,-72,1,-40)
                    backBtn.BackgroundColor3 = currentColorScheme.modeBtnBg
                    backBtn.BorderSizePixel = 0
                    backBtn.Text = "BACK"
                    backBtn.TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(234, 231, 224)
                    backBtn.Font = Enum.Font.GothamBlack
                    backBtn.TextSize = 10
                    backBtn.AutoButtonColor = false
                    backBtn.ZIndex = 123
                    mkCorner(backBtn, 12)

                    -- The CUSTOM variant's clothing changes at runtime, so its
                    -- resolved templates have to be thrown away when they do.
                    -- Defined here rather than at file scope: the register
                    -- budget is one spare slot and the chunk is at the limit.
                    local function resetCustomClothCache()
                        local cache = CustomSkinAssetCache[3]
                        if not cache then return end
                        cache.shirtTemplate = nil
                        cache.pantsTemplate = nil
                        cache.hatTemplate = nil
                        cache.lastAttempt = 0
                    end

                    -- selectSkinVariant is built further down, once the
                    -- preview plumbing exists. Forward-declared so the tab
                    -- buttons can switch variants.
                    local selectSkinVariant

                    local previewCard = Instance.new("Frame", panel)
                    previewCard.Size = UDim2.new(1,-28,1,-158)
                    previewCard.Position = UDim2.new(0,14,0,94)
                    previewCard.BackgroundColor3 = currentColorScheme.rowBg
                    previewCard.BorderSizePixel = 0
                    previewCard.ClipsDescendants = true
                    previewCard.ZIndex = 122
                    mkCorner(previewCard, 17)
                    local previewGradient = Instance.new("UIGradient", previewCard)
                    previewGradient.Color = ColorSequence.new({
                        ColorSequenceKeypoint.new(0, currentColorScheme.topBg),
                        ColorSequenceKeypoint.new(0.52, currentColorScheme.mainDark),
                        ColorSequenceKeypoint.new(1, currentColorScheme.stackBg),
                    })
                    previewGradient.Rotation = 15

                    local viewport = Instance.new("ViewportFrame", previewCard)
                    viewport.Size = UDim2.new(1,0,1,0)
                    viewport.BackgroundTransparency = 1
                    viewport.Ambient = Color3.fromRGB(186, 174, 157)
                    viewport.LightColor = Color3.fromRGB(249, 245, 238)
                    viewport.LightDirection = Vector3.new(-1,-0.5,-1)
                    viewport.ZIndex = 123

                    local previewLoading = Instance.new("TextLabel", previewCard)
                    previewLoading.Size = UDim2.new(1,-24,0,28)
                    previewLoading.Position = UDim2.new(0,12,0.5,-14)
                    previewLoading.BackgroundTransparency = 1
                    previewLoading.Text = "LOADING AVATAR..."
                    previewLoading.TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(246, 237, 197)
                    previewLoading.Font = Enum.Font.GothamBlack
                    previewLoading.TextSize = 11
                    previewLoading.ZIndex = 125

                    local statusBadge = Instance.new("TextLabel", previewCard)
                    statusBadge.Size = UDim2.new(0,90,0,26)
                    statusBadge.Position = UDim2.new(1,-100,0,10)
                    statusBadge.BorderSizePixel = 0
                    statusBadge.TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(255, 255, 255)
                    statusBadge.Font = Enum.Font.GothamBlack
                    statusBadge.TextSize = 10
                    statusBadge.ZIndex = 126
                    mkCorner(statusBadge, 13)

                    local applyBtn = Instance.new("TextButton", panel)
                    applyBtn.Size = UDim2.new(0.5,-20,0,42)
                    applyBtn.Position = UDim2.new(0,14,1,-56)
                    applyBtn.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(174, 143, 9)
                    applyBtn.BorderSizePixel = 0
                    applyBtn.Text = "APPLY SKIN"
                    applyBtn.TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(255, 255, 255)
                    applyBtn.Font = Enum.Font.GothamBlack
                    applyBtn.TextSize = 11
                    applyBtn.ZIndex = 123
                    mkCorner(applyBtn, 14)

                    local removeBtn = Instance.new("TextButton", panel)
                    removeBtn.Size = UDim2.new(0.5,-20,0,42)
                    removeBtn.Position = UDim2.new(0.5,6,1,-56)
                    removeBtn.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(76, 71, 64)
                    removeBtn.BorderSizePixel = 0
                    removeBtn.Text = "REMOVE"
                    removeBtn.TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(234, 231, 224)
                    removeBtn.Font = Enum.Font.GothamBlack
                    removeBtn.TextSize = 11
                    removeBtn.ZIndex = 123
                    mkCorner(removeBtn, 14)

                    activeSkinPopup = {root = dim}

                    -- Has a custom fit been saved yet?
                    local function customClothExists()
                        return tostring(_G._VRTCustomClothShirt or "") ~= ""
                            or tostring(_G._VRTCustomClothPants or "") ~= ""
                    end

                    local editorOpen = false
                    local function refreshVariantButtons()
                        local selected = tonumber(State.customSkinVariant) or 1
                        if selected ~= 2 and selected ~= 3 then selected = 1 end
                        -- While the editor is open the CUSTOM tab stays lit.
                        if editorOpen then selected = 3 end
                        local activeColor = currentColorScheme.main
                        local idleColor = currentColorScheme.modeBtnBg
                        local tabButtons = {skinOneButton, skinTwoButton, skinCustomButton}
                        for index, button in ipairs(tabButtons) do
                            local active = selected == index
                            button.BackgroundColor3 = active and activeColor or idleColor
                            -- Idle tabs read light-on-dark, the active one
                            -- dark-on-gold, matching the stack buttons.
                            button.TextColor3 = active
                                and (currentColorScheme.buttonText or Color3.fromRGB(28, 23, 6))
                                or (currentColorScheme.mainLight or Color3.fromRGB(255, 232, 138))
                            button.TextTransparency = 0
                            local gradient = tabGradients[index]
                            if gradient then
                                gradient.Color = adaptiveAccentSequence(currentColorScheme, active)
                            end
                        end
                        if not editorOpen then
                            subtitle.Text = CUSTOM_SKIN_VARIANTS[selected].name .. " • LIVE AVATAR PREVIEW"
                        end
                    end

                    -- Swaps between the avatar preview and the id editor.
                    local function setEditorOpen(open)
                        editorOpen = open == true
                        editor.Visible = editorOpen
                        previewCard.Visible = not editorOpen
                        if editorOpen then
                            -- Always open on whatever is currently saved.
                            shirtBox.Text = tostring(_G._VRTCustomClothShirt or "")
                            pantsBox.Text = tostring(_G._VRTCustomClothPants or "")
                            subtitle.Text = "CUSTOM FIT • ENTER CLOTH IDS"
                        end
                        refreshVariantButtons()
                    end

                    selectSkinVariant = function(index)
                        index = tonumber(index) or 1
                        if index ~= 2 and index ~= 3 then index = 1 end
                        -- CUSTOM with nothing saved opens the editor instead
                        -- of an empty preview.
                        if index == 3 and not customClothExists() then
                            setEditorOpen(true)
                            return
                        end
                        setEditorOpen(false)
                        if State.customSkinEnabled and LP.Character then
                            -- Clear the previous variant's body tint before the
                            -- new description is applied (accent must never leak
                            -- into Bart Skin's yellow body).
                            applyCustomSkinGreen(LP.Character, false)
                        end
                        State.customSkinVariant = index
                        syncCustomSkinVariant(State.customSkinVariant)
                        if State.customSkinEnabled then applyCharterToChar(LP.Character) end
                        refreshVariantButtons()
                        if activeSkinPopup and activeSkinPopup.sync then activeSkinPopup.sync() end
                        if activeSkinPopup and activeSkinPopup.refreshPreview then activeSkinPopup.refreshPreview() end
                        requestSave()
                    end

                    -- SAVE inside the editor: stores the ids, closes the
                    -- editor and lands on the CUSTOM tab wearing the new fit.
                    local function saveCustomCloth()
                        local shirt = tostring(shirtBox.Text or ""):match("(%d%d%d%d%d+)")
                        local pants = tostring(pantsBox.Text or ""):match("(%d%d%d%d%d+)")
                        if not shirt and not pants then
                            if _G._VRTToast then pcall(_G._VRTToast, "ENTER A SHIRT OR PANTS ID") end
                            return
                        end
                        local changed = tostring(_G._VRTCustomClothShirt or "") ~= (shirt or "")
                            or tostring(_G._VRTCustomClothPants or "") ~= (pants or "")
                        _G._VRTCustomClothShirt = shirt or ""
                        _G._VRTCustomClothPants = pants or ""
                        if changed then resetCustomClothCache() end
                        if State.customSkinEnabled and LP.Character then
                            applyCustomSkinGreen(LP.Character, false)
                        end
                        State.customSkinVariant = 3
                        syncCustomSkinVariant(3)
                        if State.customSkinEnabled then applyCharterToChar(LP.Character) end
                        setEditorOpen(false)
                        if activeSkinPopup and activeSkinPopup.sync then activeSkinPopup.sync() end
                        if activeSkinPopup and activeSkinPopup.refreshPreview then activeSkinPopup.refreshPreview() end
                        requestSave()
                        if _G._VRTToast then
                            pcall(_G._VRTToast, changed and "CUSTOM FIT SAVED" or "CUSTOM FIT UP TO DATE")
                        end
                    end

                    skinOneButton.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function() selectSkinVariant(1) end)
                    skinTwoButton.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function() selectSkinVariant(2) end)
                    skinCustomButton.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function() selectSkinVariant(3) end)
                    editBtn.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function() setEditorOpen(true) end)
                    saveBtn.MouseButton1Click:Connect(saveCustomCloth)
                    backBtn.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
                        setEditorOpen(false)
                        if activeSkinPopup and activeSkinPopup.refreshPreview then activeSkinPopup.refreshPreview() end
                    end)
                    shirtBox.FocusLost:Connect(function(enter) if enter then saveCustomCloth() end end)
                    pantsBox.FocusLost:Connect(function(enter) if enter then saveCustomCloth() end end)
                    refreshVariantButtons()
                    local popupDragging, popupDragInput, popupDragStart, popupStartPos = false, nil, nil, nil
                    header.InputBegan:Connect(function(input)
                        if input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton1
                            or input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.Touch then
                            popupDragging = true
                            popupDragStart = input.Position
                            popupStartPos = panel.Position
                            input.Changed:Connect(function()
                                if input.UserInputState == Enum.UserInputState.End then popupDragging = false end
                            end)
                        end
                    end)
                    header.InputChanged:Connect(function(input)
                        if input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseMovement
                            or input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.Touch then
                            popupDragInput = input
                        end
                    end)
                    activeSkinPopup.dragConn = UIS.InputChanged:Connect(function(input)
                        if input == popupDragInput and popupDragging and panel.Parent then
                            local delta = input.Position - popupDragStart
                            panel.Position = UDim2.new(
                                popupStartPos.X.Scale, popupStartPos.X.Offset + delta.X,
                                popupStartPos.Y.Scale, popupStartPos.Y.Offset + delta.Y
                            )
                        end
                    end)
                    local previewRefreshToken = 0
                    activeSkinPopup.refreshPreview = function()
                        -- The avatar preview is hidden behind the id editor;
                        -- skip rendering while it is open.
                        if editorOpen then return end
                        previewRefreshToken = previewRefreshToken + 1
                        local token = previewRefreshToken
                        previewLoading.Visible = true
                        previewLoading.Text = "LOADING AVATAR..."
                        task.spawn(function()
                            task.wait()
                            if token ~= previewRefreshToken or not viewport.Parent then return end
                            local ok, rendered = pcall(renderCustomSkinPreview, viewport, State.customSkinVariant)
                            if token == previewRefreshToken and previewLoading.Parent then
                                previewLoading.Visible = not (ok and rendered == true)
                                if previewLoading.Visible then previewLoading.Text = "PREVIEW UNAVAILABLE" end
                            end
                        end)
                    end
                    activeSkinPopup.sync = function()
                        local on = State.customSkinEnabled == true
                        local selected = tonumber(State.customSkinVariant) or 1
                        if selected ~= 2 and selected ~= 3 then selected = 1 end
                        statusBadge.Text = on and "EQUIPPED" or CUSTOM_SKIN_VARIANTS[selected].name
                        statusBadge.BackgroundColor3 = on and currentColorScheme.main or currentColorScheme.mainDark
                        applyBtn.BackgroundColor3 = on and currentColorScheme.mainDark or currentColorScheme.main
                        removeBtn.BackgroundColor3 = on and Color3.fromRGB(205, 167, 52) or currentColorScheme.modeBtnBg
                        refreshVariantButtons()
                    end
                    _G._VRTRefreshCustomSkinTheme = activeSkinPopup.sync
                    activeSkinPopup.sync()

                    closeBtn.MouseButton1Click:Connect(closeSkinPopup)
                    dim.MouseButton1Click:Connect(closeSkinPopup)
                    applyBtn.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function() setCustomSkinEnabled(true) end)
                    removeBtn.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function() setCustomSkinEnabled(false) end)
                    activeSkinPopup.refreshPreview()
                end

                skinOpen.MouseButton1Click:Connect(openSkinPopup)
            end

            State.outfit1Applied = false
            State.outfit2Applied = false
            State.outfit3Applied = false
            if false then -- legacy outfit UI removed

            -- ── NEW: Outfit Changer ──────────────────────────────
            do
                local row = Instance.new("Frame", currentPage)
                row.Size = UDim2.new(1,-16,0,42)

                semiClick.Position = UDim2.new(0.5,0,0,0)
                semiClick.BackgroundTransparency = 1
                semiClick.Text = ""; semiClick.AutoButtonColor = false
                semiClick.ZIndex = 8

                local function setMode(mode)
                    if mode~="Semi" then mode="Normal" end
                    if autoGrabModule then
                        local oldMode = autoGrabModule.getMode()
                        local oldRadii = autoGrabModule.getRadii()
                        oldRadii[oldMode] = Steal.StealRadius
                    end
                    if autoGrabModule then autoGrabModule.setMode(mode) end
                    local radii = autoGrabModule and autoGrabModule.getRadii() or {Normal=60,Semi=10}
                    Steal.StealRadius = radii[mode] or (mode=="Semi" and 10 or 75)
                    if stealRadBox then stealRadBox.Text = tostring(Steal.StealRadius) end
                    local onSemi = mode=="Semi"
                    TweenService:Create(slide, TweenInfo.new(0.18), {
                        Position = onSemi and UDim2.new(0.5,-1,0,4) or UDim2.new(0,4,0,4)
                    }):Play()
                    TweenService:Create(normalTxt, TweenInfo.new(0.14), {
                        TextTransparency = onSemi and 0.25 or 0
                    }):Play()
                    TweenService:Create(semiTxt, TweenInfo.new(0.14), {
                        TextTransparency = onSemi and 0 or 0.25
                    }):Play()
                    requestSave()
                end

                normalClick.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function() setMode("Normal") end)
                semiClick.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function() setMode("Semi") end)

                local initMode = autoGrabModule and autoGrabModule.getMode() or "Normal"
                if initMode=="Semi" then
                    slide.Position = UDim2.new(0.5,-1,0,4)
                    normalTxt.TextTransparency = 0.25
                else
                    slide.Position = UDim2.new(0,4,0,4)
                    semiTxt.TextTransparency = 0.25
                end

                _G._VRTStealModeSetUI = setMode
            end

            makeGap(4)
            makeSectionHeader("Grab Config")
            makeGap(2)

            stealRadBox = makeInputRow("Grab Radius", Steal.StealRadius, function(n)
                if n and n >= 1 and n <= 200 then
                    local applied = math.clamp(n,1,200)
                    Steal.StealRadius = applied
                    if stealRadBox then stealRadBox.Text = tostring(applied) end
                    if autoGrabModule then autoGrabModule.setRadius(applied) end
                    requestSave()
                end
            end)

            -- ── NEW: Auto Switch Carry Speed ─────────────────────────────
            makeGap(8)
            makeSectionHeader("Automatic Carry")
            makeGap(2)

            local softStealToggle = makeToggleRow("Auto Carry Switch", State.softStealEnabled or false, function(on)
                State.softStealEnabled = on
                if on then
                    if autoGrabModule and autoGrabModule.startSoftStealScanner then
                        autoGrabModule.startSoftStealScanner()
                    end
                else
                    if autoGrabModule and autoGrabModule.stopSoftStealScanner then
                        autoGrabModule.stopSoftStealScanner()
                    end
                end
                if stackBtnRefs.autoCarry then stackBtnRefs.autoCarry.setOn(on) end
                requestSave()
            end)
            toggleSetters["softSteal"] = softStealToggle

            makeGap(2)
            softStealRadiusBox = makeInputRow("Switch Radius", State.softStealRadius or 10, function(n)
                if n and n >= 1 and n <= 200 then State.softStealRadius = n; requestSave() end
            end)
            makeGap(2)
            softStealSpeedBox = makeInputRow("Switch Speed", State.softStealSpeed or 30, function(n)
                if n and n>0 then State.softStealSpeed = n end
            end)

        end)
        page.LayoutOrder = 4
    end

            saveBtn.Text = "SAVE CONFIG"

                    pcall(function() raw = _readfile(CONFIG_FILE) end)
                end

                    exportBtn.Text = "EXPORT FAILED"
                    exportBtn.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(180, 147, 48)
                    exportBtn.TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(255, 247, 222)
                else

                local raw = tostring(importBox.Text or ""):match("^%s*(.-)%s*$")
                if raw == "" and _getclipboard then
                    local clipOk, clip = pcall(_getclipboard)
                    if clipOk and type(clip) == "string" then raw = clip:match("^%s*(.-)%s*$") end
                end
                if raw == "" and _isfile(CONFIG_IMPORT_FILE) then
                    pcall(function() raw = tostring(_readfile(CONFIG_IMPORT_FILE) or ""):match("^%s*(.-)%s*$") end)
                end

                local valid, decoded = false, nil
                if raw ~= "" and #raw <= 2000000 then
                    valid, decoded = pcall(HttpService.JSONDecode, HttpService, raw)
                    -- Accept the pre-rebrand theme tag so exported configs
                    -- from the old build still import.
                    local themeOk = decoded and (decoded.theme == "verity_yellow"
                        or decoded.theme == "sevenup_green")
                    valid = valid and type(decoded) == "table"
                        and decoded.version == CONFIG_VERSION
                        and themeOk
                end

                if not valid then
                    importBtn.Text = "INVALID CONFIG"
                    importBtn.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(180, 147, 48)
                    importBtn.TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(255, 247, 222)
                else
                    local encodedOk, normalized = pcall(HttpService.JSONEncode, HttpService, decoded)
                    if encodedOk then
                        pcall(function()
                            _writefile(CONFIG_FILE, normalized)
                            _writefile(CONFIG_BACKUP, normalized)
                        end)
                    end
                    local applied = false

                        applied = loadOk and loadResult == true
                    end
                    if applied then
                        importBox.Text = ""
                        importBtn.Text = "IMPORTED!"
                        importBtn.BackgroundColor3 = currentColorScheme.main
                        importBtn.TextColor3 = currentColorScheme.buttonText
                    else
                        importBtn.Text = "IMPORT FAILED"

                State.speedToggled=false; State.laggerMode=0; State.infJumpEnabled=true; State.antiRagdollEnabled=false; State.antiRagdollVersion="V1"
                State.antiLagEnabled=false; State.saturatedColorsEnabled=false; State.stretchedResEnabled=false

                if autoGrabModule then

                    if resetRadii then resetRadii.Normal=75; resetRadii.Semi=10 end
                    autoGrabModule.setMode("Normal")

                -- Reset Soft Steal
                State.softStealEnabled = false
                State.softStealLatched = false
                State.softStealRadius = 10
                State.softStealSpeed = 30
                if softStealRadiusBox then softStealRadiusBox.Text = "10" end
                if softStealSpeedBox then softStealSpeedBox.Text = "30" end
                if autoGrabModule and autoGrabModule.stopSoftStealScanner then
                    autoGrabModule.stopSoftStealScanner()
                end
                if toggleSetters["softSteal"] then pcall(toggleSetters["softSteal"], false) end

                Keys.speed=Enum.KeyCode.Q; Keys.guiHide=Enum.KeyCode.LeftControl
                Keys.lagger=Enum.KeyCode.Unknown; Keys.tpDown=Enum.KeyCode.Unknown; Keys.drop=Enum.KeyCode.H; Keys.aimbot=Enum.KeyCode.Unknown
                Keys.autoLeft=Enum.KeyCode.L; Keys.autoRight=Enum.KeyCode.R
                Keys.antiDesync=Enum.KeyCode.V
                currentDropType = DROP_TYPES.JUMP
                if normalBox then normalBox.Text=tostring(State.normalSpeed) end; if carryBox then carryBox.Text=tostring(State.carrySpeed) end
                if laggerBox then laggerBox.Text=tostring(State.laggerSpeed) end; if laggerCarryBox then laggerCarryBox.Text=tostring(State.laggerCarrySpeed) end
                if stealRadBox then stealRadBox.Text=tostring(Steal.StealRadius) end
                if uiScaleObj then uiScaleObj.Scale=0.9 end; if uiScaleBox then uiScaleBox.Text="0.9" end
                applyStackButtonScale(1); if buttonScaleBox then buttonScaleBox.Text="1.0" end
                applyMobileButtonShape("Square"); if _G._VRTRefreshMobileButtonShape then pcall(_G._VRTRefreshMobileButtonShape) end
                if setInfJump then pcall(setInfJump,true) end; if setAntiRag then pcall(setAntiRag,false) end
                if setMedusaCounter then pcall(setMedusaCounter,false) end; if setBatCounter then pcall(setBatCounter,false) end; if setAutoSwing then pcall(setAutoSwing,true) end
                if hideButtonsSetter then pcall(hideButtonsSetter,false) end; if lockButtonsSetter then pcall(lockButtonsSetter,false) end
                if toggleSetters["autoSteal"] then pcall(toggleSetters["autoSteal"], true) end
                if toggleSetters["antiLag"] then pcall(toggleSetters["antiLag"], false) end
                if toggleSetters["stretchedRes"] then pcall(toggleSetters["stretchedRes"], false) end
